SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties

- *.jks
- *.keystore

Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

     1	package com.prahlin.cinerific
     2	
     3	import android.app.UiModeManager
     4	import android.content.pm.ActivityInfo
     5	import android.graphics.Color
     6	import android.os.Build
     7	import android.os.Bundle
     8	import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
     9	import androidx.activity.SystemBarStyle
    10	import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
    11	import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
    12	import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat
    13	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.CinerificApp
    14	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.theme.CinerificTheme
    15	
    16	class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    17	    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
    18	        requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_FULL_SENSOR
    19	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
    20	            getSystemService(UiModeManager::class.java).setApplicationNightMode(
    21	                UiModeManager.MODE_NIGHT_YES
    22	            )
    23	        }
    24	        enableEdgeToEdge(
    25	            statusBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.TRANSPARENT),
    26	            navigationBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.BLACK),
    27	        )
    28	        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
    29	        window.statusBarColor = Color.TRANSPARENT
    30	        window.navigationBarColor = Color.BLACK
    31	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
    32	            window.navigationBarDividerColor = Color.BLACK
    33	        }
    34	        WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, window.decorView).apply {
    35	            isAppearanceLightStatusBars = false
    36	            isAppearanceLightNavigationBars = false
    37	        }
    38	        setContent {
    39	            CinerificTheme {
    40	                CinerificApp()
    41	            }
    42	        }
    43	    }
    44	}

   142	@Composable
   143	fun CinerificApp() {
   144	    var showHome by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   145	    var signedInProfile by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificProfile.Guest) }
   146	    var signInSessionId by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(0) }
   147	    var selectedLanguage by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificLanguage.English) }
   148	    var introSnapshot by rememberSaveable(stateSaver = CinerificIntroSnapshotSaver) {
   149	        mutableStateOf(CinerificIntroSnapshot())
   150	    }
   151	    var introView by remember { mutableStateOf<CinerificIntroView?>(null) }
   152	    var welcomeInputBlockedUntilMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(0L) }
   153	    var signOutLoadingVisible by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   154	
   155	    fun selectProfile(profile: CinerificProfile) {
   156	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() < welcomeInputBlockedUntilMillis) return
   157	        introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   158	        signedInProfile = profile
   159	        signInSessionId += 1
   160	        signOutLoadingVisible = false
   161	        showHome = true
   162	    }
   163	
   164	    BackHandler(enabled = !showHome && introSnapshot.activeFlowName.isNotBlank()) {
   165	        introView?.handleSystemBack()
   166	    }
   167	
   168	    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   169	        AndroidView(
   170	            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
   171	            factory = { context ->
   172	                CinerificIntroView(context).apply {
   173	                    introView = this
   174	                    onIntroSnapshotChanged = { snapshot ->
   175	                        if (introSnapshot != snapshot) {
   176	                            introSnapshot = snapshot
   177	                        }
   178	                    }
   179	                    restoreIntroSnapshot(introSnapshot)
   180	                    onAvatarSelected = ::selectProfile
   181	                }
   182	            },
   183	            update = { view ->
   184	                view.onIntroSnapshotChanged = { snapshot ->
   185	                    if (introSnapshot != snapshot) {
   186	                        introSnapshot = snapshot
   187	                    }
   188	                }
   189	                view.restoreIntroSnapshot(introSnapshot)
   190	                view.onAvatarSelected = ::selectProfile
   191	            }
   192	        )
   193	
   194	        if (showHome) {
   195	            CinerificLocalizedResources(selectedLanguage) {
   196	                CinerificMainExperience(
   197	                    signInSessionId = signInSessionId,
   198	                    signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   199	                    selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   200	                    onLanguageSelected = { selectedLanguage = it },
   201	                    onSignOutLoadingStarted = { signOutLoadingVisible = true },
   202	                    onSignOut = {
   203	                        signOutLoadingVisible = true
   204	                        welcomeInputBlockedUntilMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() + 500L
   205	                        signedInProfile = CinerificProfile.Guest
   206	                        introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   207	                        showHome = false
   208	                        signOutLoadingVisible = false
   209	                    }
   210	                )
   211	            }
   212	        }
   213	
   214	        if (signOutLoadingVisible) {
   215	            SignOutLoadingOverlay(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize())
   216	        }
   217	    }
   218	}
   219	
   220	internal enum class CinerificDestination {
   221	    Home,
   222	    Movies,
   223	    Shows,
   224	    Favorites,
   225	    Settings,
   226	    ProgramDetails
   227	}
   228	
   229	internal enum class CinerificProfile(
   230	    @DrawableRes val avatarResId: Int,
   231	    @DrawableRes val nameResId: Int
   232	) {
   233	    Steve(R.drawable.steve_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.steve_name),
   234	    Martin(R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.martin_name),
   235	    Janny(R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.janny_name),
   236	    Guest(R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.guest_name)
   237	}
   238	
   239	@Composable
   240	private fun CinerificMainExperience(
   241	    signInSessionId: Int,
   242	    signedInProfile: CinerificProfile,
   243	    selectedLanguage: CinerificLanguage,
   244	    onLanguageSelected: (CinerificLanguage) -> Unit,
   245	    onSignOutLoadingStarted: () -> Unit,
   246	    onSignOut: () -> Unit
   247	) {
   248	    var destination by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(CinerificDestination.Home) }
   249	    var detailOriginDestination by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) {
   250	        mutableStateOf(CinerificDestination.Home)
   251	    }
   252	    var selectedProgramTitle by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("Sink or Swim") }
   253	    var favoriteProgramTitles by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyList<String>()) }
   254	    var userProgramRatings by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyMap<String, Int>()) }
   255	    var catalogRouteDestinationName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   256	    var catalogRouteGenreName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   257	    var catalogRouteModeName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   258	    var favoritesFullPromptRequestId by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
   259	    var autoLogoutEnabled by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   260	    var userInitiatedPlaybackActive by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   261	    var lastInteractionMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(SystemClock.uptimeMillis()) }
   262	    var primaryDestinationCanScroll by remember(destination) { mutableStateOf<Boolean?>(null) }
   263	    var portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction by remember(signInSessionId) { mutableFloatStateOf(1f) }
   264	    var portraitBottomNavHeightPx by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(1f) }
   265	    var showSignOutConfirmation by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(false) }
   266	    var signOutInProgress by remember(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(false) }
   267	    val destinationStateHolder = rememberSaveableStateHolder()
   268	    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
   269	    val configuration = LocalConfiguration.current
   270	    val isPortrait = configuration.screenHeightDp > configuration.screenWidthDp
   271	    val usesScrollLinkedPortraitBottomNav = destination in setOf(
   272	        CinerificDestination.Home,
   273	        CinerificDestination.Movies,
   274	        CinerificDestination.Shows,
   275	        CinerificDestination.Favorites,
   276	        CinerificDestination.Settings
   277	    )
   278	    val portraitBottomNavScrollConnection = remember(
   279	        isPortrait,
   280	        destination,
   281	        primaryDestinationCanScroll,
   282	        portraitBottomNavHeightPx
   283	    ) {
   284	        object : NestedScrollConnection {
   285	            override fun onPostScroll(
   286	                consumed: Offset,
   287	                available: Offset,
   288	                source: NestedScrollSource
   289	            ): Offset {
   290	                if (
   291	                    isPortrait &&
   292	                    usesScrollLinkedPortraitBottomNav &&
   293	                    primaryDestinationCanScroll == true &&
   294	                    consumed.y != 0f
   295	                ) {
   296	                    portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction = (
   297	                        portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction +
   298	                            consumed.y * PORTRAIT_BOTTOM_NAV_SCROLL_MULTIPLIER /
   299	                            portraitBottomNavHeightPx
   300	                        ).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   301	                }
   302	                return Offset.Zero
   303	            }
   304	        }
   305	    }
   306	
   307	    LaunchedEffect(destination, isPortrait, primaryDestinationCanScroll) {
   308	        portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction = if (
   309	            isPortrait &&
   310	            usesScrollLinkedPortraitBottomNav &&
   311	            primaryDestinationCanScroll != false
   312	        ) {
   313	            1f
   314	        } else {
   315	            0f
   316	        }
   317	    }
   318	    val playbackSessionController = remember(userInitiatedPlaybackActive) {
   319	        CinerificPlaybackSessionController(
   320	            isUserInitiatedPlaybackActive = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   321	            onUserInitiatedPlaybackStarted = {
   322	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = true
   323	            },
   324	            finishUserInitiatedPlayback = {
   325	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = false
   326	                lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   327	            }
   328	        )
   329	    }
   330	    val catalogRoute = remember(
   331	        catalogRouteDestinationName,
   332	        catalogRouteGenreName,
   333	        catalogRouteModeName
   334	    ) {
   335	        cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   336	            destinationName = catalogRouteDestinationName,
   337	            genreName = catalogRouteGenreName,
   338	            modeName = catalogRouteModeName
   339	        )
   340	    }
   341	
   342	    fun clearCatalogRoute() {
   343	        catalogRouteDestinationName = ""
   344	        catalogRouteGenreName = ""
   345	        catalogRouteModeName = ""
   346	    }
   347	
   348	    LaunchedEffect(autoLogoutEnabled, userInitiatedPlaybackActive, lastInteractionMillis) {
   349	        if (!autoLogoutEnabled || userInitiatedPlaybackActive) return@LaunchedEffect
   350	        delay(AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS)
   351	        val inactiveForMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - lastInteractionMillis
   352	        if (
   353	            autoLogoutEnabled &&
   354	            !userInitiatedPlaybackActive &&
   355	            inactiveForMillis >= AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS
   356	        ) {
   357	            onSignOut()
   358	        }
   359	    }
   360	
   361	    fun showProgramDetails(title: String) {
   362	        if (destination != CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails) {
   363	            detailOriginDestination = destination
   364	        }
   365	        selectedProgramTitle = title
   366	        destination = CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails
   367	    }
   368	
   369	    fun showCatalog(route: CinerificCatalogRoute) {
   370	        catalogRouteDestinationName = route.destination.name
   371	        catalogRouteGenreName = route.genre.name
   372	        catalogRouteModeName = route.mode.name
   373	        destination = route.destination
   374	    }
   375	
   376	    fun rateProgram(title: String, rating: Int) {
   377	        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings + (title to rating.coerceIn(1, 5))
   378	    }
   379	
   380	    fun toggleFavoriteProgram(title: String) {
   381	        if (title in favoriteProgramTitles) {
   382	            favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles - title
   383	            return
   384	        }
   385	
   386	        val isShow = cinerificProgramIsShow(title)
   387	        val filledSlots = cinerificFilledFavoritePlaceholderCount(
   388	            titles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   389	            isShow = isShow
   390	        )
   391	        if (filledSlots >= CINERIFIC_FAVORITES_CAPACITY) {
   392	            favoritesFullPromptRequestId += 1
   393	            return
   394	        }
   395	
   396	        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles + title
   397	    }
   398	
   399	    BackHandler {
   400	        when {
   401	            signOutInProgress -> Unit
   402	            showSignOutConfirmation -> showSignOutConfirmation = false
   403	            destination == CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails -> {
   404	                destination = detailOriginDestination
   405	            }
   406	            destination != CinerificDestination.Home -> {
   407	                clearCatalogRoute()
   408	                destination = CinerificDestination.Home
   409	            }
   410	            else -> showSignOutConfirmation = true
   411	        }
   412	    }
   413	
   414	    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalCinerificPlaybackSessionController provides playbackSessionController) {
   415	        Box(
   416	            modifier = Modifier
   417	                .fillMaxSize()
   418	                .nestedScroll(portraitBottomNavScrollConnection)
   419	                .pointerInput(Unit) {
   420	                    awaitPointerEventScope {
   421	                        while (true) {
   422	                            awaitPointerEvent(PointerEventPass.Initial)
   423	                            lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   424	                        }
   425	                    }
   426	                }
   427	        ) {
   428	            destinationStateHolder.SaveableStateProvider(destination) {
   429	                when (destination) {
   430	                    CinerificDestination.Home -> CinerificHomeScreen(
   431	                        onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   432	                        onCatalogSelected = ::showCatalog,
   433	                        onVerticalScrollabilityChanged = { primaryDestinationCanScroll = it },
   434	                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   435	                    )
   436	                    CinerificDestination.Movies,
   437	                    CinerificDestination.Shows,
   438	                    CinerificDestination.Favorites,
   439	                    CinerificDestination.Settings -> CinerificDestinationScreen(
   440	                        destination = destination,
   441	                        signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   442	                        selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   443	                        onLanguageSelected = onLanguageSelected,
   444	                        autoLogoutEnabled = autoLogoutEnabled,
   445	                        onAutoLogoutEnabledChange = { enabled ->
   446	                            autoLogoutEnabled = enabled
   447	                            lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   448	                        },
   449	                        onSignOut = onSignOut,
   450	                        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   451	                        onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   452	                        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   453	                        onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   454	                        onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   455	                        catalogRoute = catalogRoute?.takeIf { it.destination == destination },
   456	                        onVerticalScrollabilityChanged = { primaryDestinationCanScroll = it },
   457	                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   458	                    )
   459	                    CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails -> CinerificProgramDetailsScreen(
   460	                        programTitle = selectedProgramTitle,
   461	                        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   462	                        onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   463	                        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   464	                        onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   465	                        onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   466	                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   467	                    )
   468	                }
   469	            }
   470	
   471	            CinerificRightSideNavBar(
   472	                currentDestination = destination,
   473	                portraitHiddenFraction = portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction,
   474	                onPortraitBarHeightChanged = { heightPx ->
   475	                    portraitBottomNavHeightPx = heightPx.coerceAtLeast(1f)
   476	                },
   477	                onDestinationSelected = {
   478	                    clearCatalogRoute()
   479	                    destination = it
   480	                },
   481	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   482	            )
   483	
   484	            FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   485	                requestId = favoritesFullPromptRequestId,
   486	                currentDestination = destination,
   487	                suppressed = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   488	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   489	            )
   490	
   491	            if (showSignOutConfirmation) {
   492	                SignOutConfirmationOverlay(
   493	                    onConfirm = {
   494	                        showSignOutConfirmation = false
   495	                        signOutInProgress = true
   496	                        onSignOutLoadingStarted()
   497	                        scope.launch {
   498	                            delay(SIGN_OUT_LOADING_MS)
   499	                            onSignOut()
   500	                        }
   501	                    },
   502	                    onDismiss = { showSignOutConfirmation = false },
   503	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   504	                )
   505	            }
   506	        }
   507	    }
   508	}
   509	
   510	@Composable
   511	private fun SignOutLoadingOverlay(modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
   512	    Box(
   513	        modifier = modifier
   514	            .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.72f))
   515	            .clickable(onClick = {}),
   516	        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
   517	    ) {
   518	        CinerificLoadingSpinner(
   519	            modifier = Modifier.requiredSize(
   520	                width = 150.dp,
   521	                height = 150.dp *
   522	                    CINERIFIC_LOADING_SPINNER_CANVAS_HEIGHT /
   523	                    CINERIFIC_LOADING_SPINNER_CANVAS_WIDTH
   524	            )
   525	        )
   526	    }
   527	}

   529	@Composable
   530	private fun SignOutConfirmationOverlay(
   531	    onConfirm: () -> Unit,
   532	    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
   533	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   534	) {
   535	    val frameShape = RoundedCornerShape(15.dp)
   536	    val panelShape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)
   537	    val frameStrokeWidth = 2.dp
   538	    val frameClearSpace = frameStrokeWidth * (10f / 3f)
   539	    val selectionStrokeAlpha = rememberCinerificSelectionStrokeAlpha()
   540	    val selectionStrokeColor = lerpColor(
   541	        Color(0xFFC86BE0),
   542	        Color(0xFFE7E7E7),
   543	        ((selectionStrokeAlpha - 0.5f) / 0.17f).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   544	    )
   545	
   546	    Box(
   547	        modifier = modifier,
   548	        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
   549	    ) {
   550	        Box(
   551	            modifier = Modifier
   552	                .fillMaxSize()
   553	                .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.72f))
   554	                .clickable(onClick = {})
   555	        )
   556	        Box(
   557	            modifier = Modifier
   558	                .fillMaxWidth(0.84f)
   559	                .widthIn(max = 420.dp)
   560	                .border(
   561	                    frameStrokeWidth,
   562	                    selectionStrokeColor,
   563	                    frameShape
   564	                )
   565	                .padding(frameClearSpace)
   566	        ) {
   567	            Column(
   568	                modifier = Modifier
   569	                    .fillMaxWidth()
   570	                    .clip(panelShape)
   571	                    .background(Color(0xFF252126))
   572	                    .padding(horizontal = 24.dp, vertical = 22.dp),
   573	                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
   574	                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(20.dp)
   575	            ) {
   576	                Text(
   577	                    text = "Sign out from\nCinerific?",
   578	                    color = Color.White,
   579	                    fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
   580	                    fontSize = 26.sp,
   581	                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
   582	                    lineHeight = 34.sp,
   583	                    letterSpacing = 0.55.sp,
   584	                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center
   585	                )
   586	                Row(
   587	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
   588	                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
   589	                ) {
   590	                    SignOutConfirmationButton(
   591	                        label = "Yes",
   592	                        backgroundColor = ColorFrame2Background,
   593	                        contentColor = Color.White,
   594	                        borderColor = Color(0xFFC86BE0),
   595	                        onClick = onConfirm,
   596	                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
   597	                    )
   598	                    SignOutConfirmationButton(
   599	                        label = "No",
   600	                        backgroundColor = Color(0xFF3A343C),
   601	                        contentColor = Color.White,
   602	                        borderColor = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.72f),
   603	                        onClick = onDismiss,
   604	                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
   605	                    )
   606	                }
   607	            }
   608	        }
   609	    }
   610	}

   612	@Composable
   613	private fun SignOutConfirmationButton(
   614	    label: String,
   615	    backgroundColor: Color,
   616	    contentColor: Color,
   617	    borderColor: Color,
   618	    onClick: () -> Unit,
   619	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   620	) {
   621	    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(6.dp)
   622	    Box(
   623	        modifier = modifier
   624	            .height(52.dp)
   625	            .clip(shape)
   626	            .background(backgroundColor)
   627	            .border(1.dp, borderColor, shape)
   628	            .clickable(onClick = onClick),
   629	        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
   630	    ) {
   631	        Text(
   632	            text = label,
   633	            color = contentColor,
   634	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
   635	            fontSize = 20.sp,
   636	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   637	            letterSpacing = 0.55.sp
   638	        )
   639	    }
   640	}
   641	
   642	private fun cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   643	    destinationName: String,
   644	    genreName: String,
   645	    modeName: String
   646	): CinerificCatalogRoute? {
   647	    if (destinationName.isBlank() || genreName.isBlank() || modeName.isBlank()) return null
   648	
   649	    val destination = CinerificDestination.values().firstOrNull { it.name == destinationName } ?: return null
   650	    val genre = ViewportGenre.values().firstOrNull { it.name == genreName } ?: return null
   651	    val mode = ViewportMode.values().firstOrNull { it.name == modeName } ?: return null
   652	
   653	    return CinerificCatalogRoute(
   654	        destination = destination,
   655	        genre = genre,
   656	        mode = mode
   657	    )
   658	}
   659	
   660	@Composable
   661	private fun FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   662	    requestId: Int,
   663	    currentDestination: CinerificDestination,
   664	    suppressed: Boolean,
   665	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   666	) {
   667	    var visible by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   668	
   669	    LaunchedEffect(requestId, suppressed) {
   670	        if (suppressed) {
   671	            visible = false
   672	            return@LaunchedEffect
   673	        }
   674	        if (requestId == 0) {
   675	            visible = false
   676	            return@LaunchedEffect
   677	        }
   678	        visible = true
   679	        delay(FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_VISIBLE_MS)
   680	        visible = false
   681	    }
   682	
   683	    val alpha by animateFloatAsState(
   684	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0f,
   685	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   686	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-alpha"
   687	    )
   688	    val promptScale by animateFloatAsState(
   689	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0.94f,
   690	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   691	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-scale"
   692	    )
   693	
   694	    if (suppressed || requestId == 0 || (!visible && alpha <= 0.01f)) return
   695	
   696	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = modifier) {
   697	        val stageScale = maxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
   698	        val isPortraitDetail = currentDestination == CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails &&
   699	            maxHeight > maxWidth
   700	        val promptY = if (isPortraitDetail) {
   701	            DETAIL_HERO_LOGO_CENTER_Y - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT / 2f
   702	        } else {
   703	            FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_Y
   704	        }
   705	        FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   706	            stageScale = stageScale,
   707	            modifier = Modifier
   708	                .absoluteOffset(
   709	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_X * stageScale).dp,
   710	                    y = (promptY * stageScale).dp
   711	                )
   712	                .graphicsLayer {
   713	                    this.alpha = alpha
   714	                    scaleX = promptScale
   715	                    scaleY = promptScale
   716	                    transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0.5f, 0f)
   717	                }
   718	        )
   719	    }
   720	}
   721	
   722	@Composable
   723	private fun FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   724	    stageScale: Float,
   725	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   726	) {
   727	    Box(
   728	        modifier = modifier.requiredSize(
   729	            width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
   730	            height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
   731	        )
   732	    ) {
   733	        Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   734	            val xScale = size.width / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH
   735	            val yScale = size.height / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT
   736	            val pointer = Path().apply {
   737	                moveTo(47.5f * xScale, 0f)
   738	                lineTo(63f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   739	                lineTo(32f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   740	                close()
   741	            }
   742	            drawPath(pointer, ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent)
   743	            drawRoundRect(
   744	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptFill,
   745	                topLeft = Offset(0f, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale),
   746	                size = Size(
   747	                    width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * xScale,
   748	                    height = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT * yScale
   749	                ),
   750	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
   751	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
   752	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
   753	                )
   754	            )
   755	            drawRoundRect(
   756	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
   757	                topLeft = Offset(
   758	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * xScale / 2f,
   759	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale +
   760	                        FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * yScale / 2f
   761	                ),
   762	                size = Size(
   763	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * xScale,
   764	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * yScale
   765	                ),
   766	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
   767	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
   768	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
   769	                ),
   770	                style = Stroke(width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * min(xScale, yScale))
   771	            )
   772	        }
   773	        Text(
   774	            text = "You've\nfilled\nup on\nfavorites!",
   775	            color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
   776	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
   777	            fontSize = (14f * stageScale).sp,
   778	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   779	            lineHeight = (21.252f * stageScale).sp,
   780	            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
   781	            modifier = Modifier
   782	                .absoluteOffset(
   783	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_X * stageScale).dp,
   784	                    y = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_Y * stageScale).dp
   785	                )
   786	                .requiredSize(
   787	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
   788	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
   789	                )
   790	        )
   791	    }
   792	}
   793	
   794	@Composable
   795	private fun BootIntroFromFigma(progress: Float) {
   796	    val p = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   797	    val images = rememberIntroImages()
   798	    val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   799	        p,
   800	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   801	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   802	    )
   803	    val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(p, 2500, 3900)
   804	    val background = introBackgroundBrush(
   805	        solidProgress = blackToPurple,
   806	        gradientProgress = purpleToSettle
   807	    )
   808	    val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(p, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   809	    val logoEntryProgress = bouncySegmentMs(p, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
   810	    val logoFinalProgress = easedSegmentMs(p, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   811	    val avatarSettleAlpha = easedSegmentMs(p, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   812	    val avatarYOffset = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarSettleAlpha)
   813	
   814	    Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   815	        val stageScale = min(size.width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, size.height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
   816	        val stageLeft = (size.width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f
   817	        val stageTop = (size.height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f
   818	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY(size.width > size.height)
   819	
   820	        drawRect(brush = background, size = size)
   821	
   822	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   823	            val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
   824	            val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
   825	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   826	                end = baseBounds,
   827	                amount = logoEntryProgress
   828	            )
   829	            val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
   830	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   831	                end = baseBounds,
   832	                amount = logoEntryProgress
   833	            )
   834	            val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
   835	
   836	            drawFigmaImage(
   837	                image = images.logoSimple,
   838	                bounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
   839	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
   840	                stageTop = stageTop,
   841	                stageScale = stageScale,
   842	                alpha = logoAlpha
   843	            )
   844	            drawFigmaImage(
   845	                image = images.logoEyes,
   846	                bounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
   847	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
   848	                stageTop = stageTop,
   849	                stageScale = stageScale,
   850	                alpha = logoAlpha
   851	            )
   852	        }
   853	
   854	        if (avatarSettleAlpha > 0.01f) {
   855	            val y = avatarYOffset
   856	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveAvatar, FigmaBounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   857	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinAvatar, FigmaBounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   858	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyAvatar, FigmaBounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   859	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestAvatar, FigmaBounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   860	
   861	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveName, FigmaBounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   862	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinName, FigmaBounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   863	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyName, FigmaBounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   864	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestName, FigmaBounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   865	        }
   866	    }
   867	}

   877	    // Node 474:7311 with Logo Slide 1 instance 478:6917.

   879

   881	        slideYOffset.snapTo(-417f)
   882	        slideYOffset.animateTo(
   883	            targetValue = 0f,

  1060	
  1061	private fun DrawScope.drawFigmaImage(
  1062	    image: ImageBitmap,
  1063	    bounds: FigmaBounds,
  1064	    stageLeft: Float,
  1065	    stageTop: Float,
  1066	    stageScale: Float,
  1067	    alpha: Float = 1f,
  1068	    clipCircle: Boolean = false
  1069	) {
  1070	    val left = stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale
  1071	    val top = stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale
  1072	    val width = bounds.w * stageScale
  1073	    val height = bounds.h * stageScale
  1074	
  1075	    if (clipCircle) {
  1076	        val clip = Path().apply {
  1077	            addOval(Rect(left, top, left + width, top + height))
  1078	        }
  1079	        clipPath(clip) {
  1080	            drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
  1081	        }
  1082	    } else {
  1083	        drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
  1084	    }
  1085	}
  1086	
  1087	private fun DrawScope.drawImageBitmap(
  1088	    image: ImageBitmap,
  1089	    left: Float,
  1090	    top: Float,
  1091	    width: Float,
  1092	    height: Float,
  1093	    alpha: Float
  1094	) {
  1095	    drawImage(
  1096	        image = image,
  1097	        dstOffset = IntOffset(left.roundToInt(), top.roundToInt()),
  1098	        dstSize = IntSize(width.roundToInt(), height.roundToInt()),
  1099	        alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1100	    )
  1101	}

  1103	@Composable
  1104	private fun SettlingLogoLayer(
  1105	    scale: Float,
  1106	    entryProgress: Float,
  1107	    finalProgress: Float,
  1108	    alpha: Float
  1109	) {
  1110	    val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
  1111	    val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
  1112	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1113	        end = baseBounds,
  1114	        amount = entryProgress
  1115	    )
  1116	    val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
  1117	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1118	        end = baseBounds,
  1119	        amount = entryProgress
  1120	    )
  1121	    val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
  1122	    val simpleBounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
  1123	    val eyesBounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
  1124	
  1125	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1126	        base = baseBounds,
  1127	        target = simpleBounds,
  1128	        scale = scale,
  1129	        resId = R.drawable.logo_simple_large,
  1130	        alpha = alpha
  1131	    )
  1132	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1133	        base = baseBounds,
  1134	        target = eyesBounds,
  1135	        scale = scale,
  1136	        resId = R.drawable.logo_eyes_large,
  1137	        alpha = alpha
  1138	    )
  1139	}

  1141	@Composable
  1142	private fun AvatarSelectionLayer(scale: Float, alpha: Float, yOffset: Float) {
  1143	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
  1144	    val configuration = LocalConfiguration.current
  1145	    val isLandscape = configuration.screenWidthDp > configuration.screenHeightDp
  1146	    val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY(isLandscape)
  1147	    val accountPromptExtraShiftY = signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(isLandscape)
  1148	    val accountPromptTextSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize(isLandscape)
  1149	    val accountPromptSecondaryTextSize = accountPromptTextSize * ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SECONDARY_TEXT_SCALE
  1150	    Box(
  1151	        modifier = Modifier
  1152	            .requiredSize(figma(1194f, scale), figma(834f, scale))
  1153	            .graphicsLayer {
  1154	                this.alpha = alpha
  1155	                translationY = yOffset * scale * density
  1156	            }
  1157	    ) {
  1158	        // Figma Logo Slide 2, Property 1=Variant2, constrained inside Sign-in Frame.
  1159	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 152f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.steve_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
  1160	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 390f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
  1161	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 628f, y = 454f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
  1162	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 866f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
  1163	
  1164	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 130f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.steve_name)
  1165	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 368f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.martin_name)
  1166	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 606f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.janny_name)
  1167	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 854f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 200f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.guest_name)
  1168	        SignInPromptText(
  1169	            text = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
  1170	            x = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LEFT_ANCHOR_X,
  1171	            y = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TOP + stackShiftY + accountPromptExtraShiftY,
  1172	            textSize = accountPromptSecondaryTextSize,
  1173	            textAlign = TextAlign.Left,
  1174	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
  1175	            scale = scale
  1176	        )
  1177	        SignInPromptText(
  1178	            text = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1179	            x = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X - ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH / 2f,
  1180	            y = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TOP + stackShiftY + accountPromptExtraShiftY,
  1181	            textSize = accountPromptTextSize,
  1182	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
  1183	            scale = scale
  1184	        )
  1185	        SignInPromptText(
  1186	            text = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
  1187	            x = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_RIGHT_ANCHOR_X - ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH,
  1188	            y = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TOP + stackShiftY + accountPromptExtraShiftY,
  1189	            textSize = accountPromptSecondaryTextSize,
  1190	            textAlign = TextAlign.Right,
  1191	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
  1192	            scale = scale
  1193	        )
  1194	    }
  1195	}
  1196	
  1197	@Composable
  1198	private fun SignInPromptText(
  1199	    text: String,
  1200	    x: Float,
  1201	    y: Float,
  1202	    textSize: Float,
  1203	    fontWeight: FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
  1204	    textAlign: TextAlign = TextAlign.Center,
  1205	    scale: Float
  1206	) {
  1207	    val contentAlignment = when (textAlign) {
  1208	        TextAlign.Left -> Alignment.CenterStart
  1209	        TextAlign.Right -> Alignment.CenterEnd
  1210	        else -> Alignment.Center
  1211	    }
  1212	    Box(
  1213	        modifier = Modifier
  1214	            .absoluteOffset(
  1215	                x = figma(x, scale),
  1216	                y = figma(y, scale)
  1217	            )
  1218	            .requiredSize(
  1219	                width = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH, scale),
  1220	                height = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_HEIGHT, scale)
  1221	            ),
  1222	        contentAlignment = contentAlignment
  1223	    ) {
  1224	        Text(
  1225	            text = text,
  1226	            color = Color.White,
  1227	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
  1228	            fontSize = (textSize * scale).sp,
  1229	            fontWeight = fontWeight,
  1230	            lineHeight = (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LINE_HEIGHT * textSize / ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * scale).sp,
  1231	            maxLines = 1,
  1232	            softWrap = false,
  1233	            textAlign = textAlign
  1234	        )
  1235	    }
  1236	}

  1239	private fun FigmaAvatarImage(
  1240	    x: Float,
  1241	    y: Float,
  1242	    w: Float,
  1243	    h: Float,
  1244	    scale: Float,
  1245	    @DrawableRes resId: Int
  1246	) {

  1248	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1249	        contentDescription = null,
  1250	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1251	        modifier = Modifier
  1252	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
  1253	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
  1254	            .clip(CircleShape)
  1255	    )
  1256	}

  1269	@Composable
  1270	private fun PromoBackgroundLayer(
  1271	    scale: Float,
  1272	    darkOverlay: Float,
  1273	    @DrawableRes backgroundResId: Int,
  1274	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1275	) {
  1276	    // Nodes 793:7049 / 793:7050 at x:0 y:0 size:1584x1584.
  1277	    FigmaAssetImage(
  1278	        x = 0f,
  1279	        y = 0f,
  1280	        w = 1584f,
  1281	        h = 1584f,
  1282	        scale = scale,
  1283	        resId = backgroundResId,
  1284	        alpha = alpha
  1285	    )
  1286	
  1287	    if (darkOverlay > 0f) {
  1288	        Box(
  1289	            modifier = Modifier
  1290	                .absoluteOffset(x = figma(0f, scale), y = figma(0f, scale))
  1291	                .requiredSize(figma(1584f, scale), figma(1584f, scale))
  1292	                .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
  1293	                .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = darkOverlay))
  1294	        )
  1295	    }
  1296	}

  1298	@Composable
  1299	private fun TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1300	    base: FigmaBounds,
  1301	    target: FigmaBounds,
  1302	    scale: Float,
  1303	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1304	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1305	) {
  1306	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
  1307	    Image(
  1308	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1309	        contentDescription = null,
  1310	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1311	        modifier = Modifier
  1312	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(base.x, scale), y = figma(base.y, scale))
  1313	            .requiredSize(figma(base.w, scale), figma(base.h, scale))
  1314	            .graphicsLayer {
  1315	                this.alpha = alpha
  1316	                transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0f, 0f)
  1317	                translationX = (target.x - base.x) * scale * density
  1318	                translationY = (target.y - base.y) * scale * density
  1319	                scaleX = target.w / base.w
  1320	                scaleY = target.h / base.h
  1321	            }
  1322	    )
  1323	}
  1324	
  1325	@Composable
  1326	private fun FigmaAssetImage(
  1327	    x: Float,
  1328	    y: Float,
  1329	    w: Float,
  1330	    h: Float,
  1331	    scale: Float,
  1332	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1333	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1334	) {
  1335	    Image(
  1336	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1337	        contentDescription = null,
  1338	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1339	        modifier = Modifier
  1340	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
  1341	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
  1342	            .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
  1343	    )
  1344	}
  1345	
  1346	@Composable
  1347	private fun FigmaStage(background: Brush, content: @Composable BoxWithConstraintsScope.(Float) -> Unit) {
  1348	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  1349	        val outerMaxWidth = this.maxWidth
  1350	        val outerMaxHeight = this.maxHeight
  1351	        val scale = min(outerMaxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, outerMaxHeight.value / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  1352	        val stageWidth = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, scale)
  1353	        val stageHeight = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT, scale)
  1354	
  1355	        Box(
  1356	            modifier = Modifier
  1357	                .fillMaxSize()
  1358	                .background(background)
  1359	        ) {
  1360	            BoxWithConstraints(
  1361	                modifier = Modifier
  1362	                    .align(Alignment.Center)
  1363	                    .requiredSize(stageWidth, stageHeight)
  1364	            ) {
  1365	                content(scale)
  1366	            }
  1367	        }
  1368	    }
  1369	}
  1370	
  1371	private fun figma(px: Float, scale: Float) = (px * scale).dp
  1372	
  1373	private fun signInStackShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1374	    return if (isLandscape) {
  1375	        SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1376	    } else {
  1377	        SIGN_IN_PORTRAIT_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1378	    }
  1379	}
  1380	
  1381	private fun signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1382	    return if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1383	}
  1384	
  1385	private fun signInAccountPromptTextSize(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1386	    return if (isLandscape) ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LANDSCAPE_TEXT_SIZE else ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE
  1387	}
  1388	
  1389	private fun introBackgroundBrush(solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float): Brush {
  1390	    val solid = lerpColor(ColorFrame1Background, ColorFrame2Background, solidProgress)
  1391	    return Brush.verticalGradient(
  1392	        colors = listOf(
  1393	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientTop, gradientProgress),
  1394	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientCenter, gradientProgress),
  1395	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientBottom, gradientProgress)
  1396	        )
  1397	    )
  1398	}
  1399	
  1400	private fun solidBrush(color: Color): Brush = Brush.verticalGradient(
  1401	    colors = listOf(color, color)
  1402	)
  1403	
  1404	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  1405	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  1406	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1407	}
  1408	
  1409	private fun easedSegment(value: Float, start: Float, end: Float): Float {
  1410	    val progress = ((value - start) / (end - start)).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1411	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(progress)
  1412	}
  1413	
  1414	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1415	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  1416	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1417	}
  1418	
  1419	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1420	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1421	}
  1422	
  1423	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1424	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1425	}
  1426	
  1427	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  1428	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1429	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  1430	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  1431	    return base + bounce
  1432	}
  1433	
  1434	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  1435	
  1436	private fun lerpBounds(start: FigmaBounds, end: FigmaBounds, amount: Float) = FigmaBounds(
  1437	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  1438	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  1439	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  1440	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  1441	)
  1442	
  1443	private val CinerificIntroSnapshotSaver = listSaver<CinerificIntroSnapshot, Any>(
  1444	    save = { snapshot ->
  1445	        listOf(
  1446	            snapshot.activeFlowName,
  1447	            snapshot.usernameText,
  1448	            snapshot.passwordText,
  1449	            snapshot.confirmPasswordText,
  1450	            snapshot.emailText,
  1451	            snapshot.monthText,
  1452	            snapshot.dayText,
  1453	            snapshot.yearText,
  1454	            snapshot.subscriptionTierText,
  1455	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName,
  1456	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName,
  1457	            snapshot.rememberMeChecked,
  1458	            snapshot.createAvatarIndex,
  1459	            snapshot.portraitProfileIndex
  1460	        )
  1461	    },
  1462	    restore = { values ->
  1463	        CinerificIntroSnapshot(
  1464	            activeFlowName = values.getOrNull(0) as? String ?: "",
  1465	            usernameText = values.getOrNull(1) as? String ?: "",
  1466	            passwordText = values.getOrNull(2) as? String ?: "",
  1467	            confirmPasswordText = values.getOrNull(3) as? String ?: "",
  1468	            emailText = values.getOrNull(4) as? String ?: "",
  1469	            monthText = values.getOrNull(5) as? String ?: "Mon",
  1470	            dayText = values.getOrNull(6) as? String ?: "Day",
  1471	            yearText = values.getOrNull(7) as? String ?: "Year",
  1472	            subscriptionTierText = values.getOrNull(8) as? String ?: "Subscription Tier",
  1473	            forgotRecoveryTargetName = values.getOrNull(9) as? String ?: "Password",
  1474	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName = values.getOrNull(10) as? String ?: "Idle",
  1475	            rememberMeChecked = values.getOrNull(11) as? Boolean ?: false,
  1476	            createAvatarIndex = values.getOrNull(12) as? Int ?: 0,
  1477	            portraitProfileIndex = values.getOrNull(13) as? Int ?: 0
  1478	        )
  1479	    }
  1480	)
