   450	    fun handleSystemBack(): Boolean {
   451	        if (activeMockFlow == null || mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress <= 0f) return false
   452	        closeMockSignInScreen()
   453	        return true
   454	    }
   455	
   456	    fun restoreIntroSnapshot(snapshot: CinerificIntroSnapshot) {
   457	        if (appliedIntroSnapshot == snapshot) return
   458	
   459	        val restoredFlow = enumValueOrNull<MockAccountFlow>(snapshot.activeFlowName)
   460	        val restoredRecoveryTarget = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotRecoveryTarget>(
   461	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName
   462	        ) ?: MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
   463	        val restoredSubmissionState = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState>(
   464	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName
   465	        ) ?: MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle
   466	
   467	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   468	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   469	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   470	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   471	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   472	        pressedMockField = null
   473	        pressedRememberMe = false
   474	        pressedBackChevron = false
   475	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   476	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   477	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   478	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   479	        lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   480	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   481	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   482	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   483	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   484	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   485	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   486	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   487	        activeComposingText = ""
   488	        expandedMockDropdown = null
   489	        focusedMockField = null
   490	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
   491	        mockInputDimActiveField = null
   492	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
   493	
   494	        activeMockFlow = restoredFlow
   495	        if (restoredFlow == null) {
   496	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
   497	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
   498	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
   499	        } else {
   500	            mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   501	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
   502	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
   503	        }
   504	
   505	        rememberMeChecked = snapshot.rememberMeChecked
   506	        usernameText = snapshot.usernameText
   507	        passwordText = snapshot.passwordText
   508	        confirmPasswordText = snapshot.confirmPasswordText
   509	        emailText = snapshot.emailText
   510	        monthText = snapshot.monthText
   511	        dayText = snapshot.dayText
   512	        yearText = snapshot.yearText
   513	        subscriptionTierText = normalizedMembershipTierSelection(snapshot.subscriptionTierText)
   514	        forgotRecoveryTarget = restoredRecoveryTarget
   515	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = restoredSubmissionState
   516	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = if (
   517	            restoredSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
   518	        ) {
   519	            SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   520	        } else {
   521	            null
   522	        }
   523	
   524	        mockCreateAvatarIndex = snapshot.createAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
   525	        portraitProfileIndex = snapshot.portraitProfileIndex.coerceIn(0, FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.lastIndex)
   526	        resetPortraitProfileCarousel()
   527	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = null
   528	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   529	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   530	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection = MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
   531	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress = 0f
   532	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets.keys.forEach { dropdown ->
   533	            mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = 0
   534	        }
   535	
   536	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
   537	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
   538	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   539	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   540	        appliedIntroSnapshot = currentIntroSnapshot()
   541	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   542	    }
   543	
   544	    override fun onSizeChanged(width: Int, height: Int, oldWidth: Int, oldHeight: Int) {
   545	        super.onSizeChanged(width, height, oldWidth, oldHeight)
   546	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   547	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   548	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   549	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   550	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   551	        pressedMockField = null
   552	        pressedRememberMe = false
   553	        pressedBackChevron = false
   554	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   555	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   556	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   557	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   558	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   559	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   560	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   561	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   562	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   563	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   564	        resetPortraitProfileCarousel()
   565	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   566	    }
   567	
   568	    override fun onApplyWindowInsets(insets: WindowInsets): WindowInsets {
   569	        val systemBars = WindowInsetsCompat.toWindowInsetsCompat(insets, this)
   570	            .getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars())
   571	        systemBarInsetTopPx = systemBars.top
   572	        systemBarInsetBottomPx = systemBars.bottom
   573	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   574	        return super.onApplyWindowInsets(insets)
   575	    }
   576	
   577	    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
   578	        super.onDraw(canvas)
   579	
   580	        if (IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis == 0L) {
   581	            IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   582	        }
   583	        val progress = bootProgressAt(IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis)
   584	        val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   585	            progress,
   586	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   587	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   588	        )
   589	        val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2500, 3900)
   590	        drawIntroBackground(canvas, blackToPurple, purpleToSettle)
   591	
   592	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return
   593	        finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded()
   594	        finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded()
   595	        finishPortraitProfileCarouselIfNeeded()
   596	        finishMockCreateAvatarCarouselIfNeeded()
   597	        finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded()
   598	        val mockProgress = mockSignInProgress()
   599	        updateMockFormFocusAnimation()
   600	        updateMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   601	        updateMockInputDimAnimation()
   602	        val formFocusMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
   603	
   604	        val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(progress, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   605	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   606	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, progress, logoAlpha)
   607	        }
   608	
   609	        val avatarAlpha = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   610	        val mockMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockProgress)
   611	        if (avatarAlpha > 0.01f) {
   612	            val stackAlpha = avatarAlpha * (1f - mockMotion)
   613	            val y = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha) + MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * mockMotion
   614	            val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
   615	            if (stackAlpha > 0.01f) {
   616	                if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
   617	                    drawPortraitProfile(canvas, stage, y, stackAlpha)
   618	                } else {
   619	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveAvatar, Bounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   620	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinAvatar, Bounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   621	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyAvatar, Bounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   622	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestAvatar, Bounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   623	
   624	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveName, Bounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   625	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinName, Bounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   626	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyName, Bounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   627	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestName, Bounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   628	                }
   629	            }
   630	        }
   631	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   632	            val outgoingFlow = outgoingMockFlow
   633	            if (outgoingFlow != null) {
   634	                val switchMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
   635	                drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   636	                    canvas,
   637	                    outgoingFlow,
   638	                    stage.left,
   639	                    stage.top,
   640	                    stage.scale,
   641	                    1f - switchMotion,
   642	                    formFocusMotion,
   643	                    updateLabelAnimation = false
   644	                )
   645	                activeMockFlow?.let { incomingFlow ->
   646	                    drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   647	                        canvas,
   648	                        incomingFlow,
   649	                        stage.left,
   650	                        stage.top,
   651	                        stage.scale,
   652	                        switchMotion,
   653	                        formFocusMotion,
   654	                        updateLabelAnimation = true
   655	                    )
   656	                }
   657	            } else {
   658	                drawMockSignInForm(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, mockMotion, formFocusMotion)
   659	            }
   660	            drawBackChevron(
   661	                canvas,
   662	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X,
   663	                mockBackChevronRowTopY(),
   664	                stage.left,
   665	                if (usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout()) {
   666	                    stage.top
   667	                } else {
   668	                    accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale)
   669	                },
   670	                stage.scale,
   671	                mockMotion
   672	            )
   673	        }
   674	        drawAccountPrompt(
   675	            canvas = canvas,
   676	            stageLeft = stage.left,
   677	            stageTop = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) stage.top else accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale),
   678	            stageScale = stage.scale,
   679	            yOffset = if (mockSignInStartMillis == null) {
   680	                lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha)
   681	            } else {
   682	                0f
   683	            },
   684	            alpha = avatarAlpha
   685	        )
   686	
   687	        if (
   688	            progress < 1f ||
   689	            isMockSignInAnimating() ||
   690	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating() ||
   691	            isMockFormLabelAnimating() ||
   692	            isMockFormFocusAnimating() ||
   693	            isMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimating() ||
   694	            isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating() ||
   695	            isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
   696	            isMockForgotPasswordSubmissionAnimating() ||
   697	            isMockInputDimAnimating()
   698	        ) {
   699	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   700	        }
   701	    }
   702	
   703	    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
   704	        return when (event.actionMasked) {
   705	            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
   706	                mockTouchDownX = event.x
   707	                mockTouchDownY = event.y
   708	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
   709	                portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
   710	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   711	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   712	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   713	                    portraitAuthScrollStartY = event.y
   714	                    portraitAuthScrollStartOffset = portraitAuthScrollY
   715	                    portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   716	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   717	                        return true
   718	                    }
   719	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   720	                    pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   721	                        settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   722	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   723	                        !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   724	                        settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   725	                    if (
   726	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   727	                        pressedSignInPrompt ||
   728	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt
   729	                    ) {
   730	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   731	                        return true
   732	                    }
   733	                    val touchedDropdownOptions = mockDropdownOptionsHit(event.x, event.y)
   734	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = touchedDropdownOptions?.takeIf { mockDropdownCanScroll(it) }
   735	                    mockDropdownScrollStartY = event.y
   736	                    mockDropdownScrollStartOffset = activeMockDropdownScroll?.let { mockDropdownScrollOffset(it) } ?: 0
   737	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = if (touchedDropdownOptions != null) {
   738	                        mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   739	                    } else {
   740	                        null
   741	                    }
   742	                    pressedMockDropdown = if (pressedMockDropdownOption == null && touchedDropdownOptions == null) {
   743	                        mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   744	                    } else {
   745	                        null
   746	                    }
   747	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = if (
   748	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   749	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   750	                        pressedMockDropdown == null
   751	                    ) {
   752	                        mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   753	                    } else {
   754	                        null
   755	                    }
   756	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   757	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   758	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   759	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   760	                        mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   761	                    val touchedCreateAvatarCarousel = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   762	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   763	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   764	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   765	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   766	                        mockCreateAvatarCarouselHit(event.x, event.y) &&
   767	                        !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
   768	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   769	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   770	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   771	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   772	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = if (
   773	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   774	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   775	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   776	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   777	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   778	                        touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   779	                    ) {
   780	                        mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   781	                    } else {
   782	                        null
   783	                    }
   784	                    pressedMockField = if (
   785	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   786	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   787	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   788	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   789	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   790	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   791	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   792	                    ) {
   793	                        mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   794	                    } else {
   795	                        null
   796	                    }
   797	                    pressedRememberMe = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   798	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   799	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   800	                        pressedMockField == null &&
   801	                        mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   802	                    pressedBackChevron = pressedMockField == null &&
   803	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   804	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   805	                        !pressedRememberMe &&
   806	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   807	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   808	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   809	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   810	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   811	                        mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   812	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   813	                    return true
   814	                }
   815	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   816	                pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   817	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   818	                    !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   819	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   820	                pressedAvatarProfile = if (
   821	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   822	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   823	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   824	                    isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()
   825	                ) {
   826	                    null
   827	                } else {
   828	                    settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   829	                }
   830	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   831	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   832	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   833	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   834	            }
   835	            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
   836	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   837	                    if (updatePortraitAuthScroll(event)) {
   838	                        return true
   839	                    }
   840	                    if (updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event)) {
   841	                        return true
   842	                    }
   843	                    if (updateMockDropdownScroll(event)) {
   844	                        return true
   845	                    }
   846	                    if (mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) == null && shouldStartMockDragReturn(event)) {
   847	                        mockDragReturnInProgress = true
   848	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   849	                    }
   850	                    return true
   851	                }
   852	                if (isPortraitIntroLayout() && pressedAvatarProfile != null) {
   853	                    val horizontalDelta = event.x - mockTouchDownX
   854	                    val verticalDelta = event.y - mockTouchDownY
   855	                    if (abs(horizontalDelta) > touchSlop && abs(horizontalDelta) > abs(verticalDelta)) {
   856	                        portraitProfileSwipeMoved = true
   857	                    }
   858	                    if (portraitProfileSwipeMoved) {
   859	                        portraitProfileDragDeltaX = horizontalDelta
   860	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   861	                    }
   862	                    return true
   863	                }
   864	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   865	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   866	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   867	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   868	            }
   869	            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
   870	                if (portraitAuthScrollMoved) {
   871	                    portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   872	                    pressedMockField = null
   873	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   874	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   875	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   876	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   877	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   878	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   879	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   880	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   881	                    return true
   882	                }
   883	                if (mockDragReturnInProgress) {
   884	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   885	                    pressedMockField = null
   886	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   887	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   888	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   889	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   890	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   891	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   892	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   893	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   894	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   895	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   896	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   897	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   898	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   899	                    return true
   900	                }
   901	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   902	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   903	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   904	                        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   905	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   906	                        return true
   907	                    }
   908	                    val requestedFlow = when {
   909	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   910	                            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
   911	                        }
   912	                        pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   913	                            MockAccountFlow.SignIn
   914	                        }
   915	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt && settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   916	                            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
   917	                        }
   918	                        else -> null
   919	                    }
   920	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   921	                    pressedSignInPrompt = false
   922	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   923	                    if (requestedFlow != null) {
   924	                        super.performClick()
   925	                        switchMockAccountFlow(requestedFlow)
   926	                        return true
   927	                    }
   928	                    val releasedDropdownOption = mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   929	                    val releasedDropdown = mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   930	                    val releasedField = mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   931	                    val releasedRememberMe = mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   932	                    val releasedBackChevron = mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   933	                    val releasedCreateAvatarNav = mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   934	                    val releasedForgotRecoveryTarget = mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   935	                    val releasedForgotPasswordSubmit = mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   936	                    val wasTouchingDropdownOptions = activeMockDropdownScroll != null ||
   937	                        mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) != null
   938	                    val wasScrollingDropdown = mockDropdownScrollMoved
   939	                    val wasPressingRememberMe = pressedRememberMe
   940	                    val wasPressingBackChevron = pressedBackChevron
   941	                    val wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget != null
   942	                    val wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit
   943	                    val wasDraggingCreateAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag
   944	                    val createAvatarDragMoved = mockCreateAvatarDragMoved
   945	                    val createAvatarDragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
   946	                    val createAvatarDragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress()
   947	                    val pressedDropdown = pressedMockDropdown
   948	                    val pressedOption = pressedMockDropdownOption
   949	                    val pressedCreateAvatarNav = pressedMockCreateAvatarNav
   950	                    val pressedForgotRecoverySelection = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget
   951	                    val shouldSelectDropdownOption = !wasScrollingDropdown &&
   952	                        pressedOption != null &&
   953	                        pressedOption == releasedDropdownOption
   954	                    val shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null &&
   955	                        pressedForgotRecoverySelection == releasedForgotRecoveryTarget
   956	                    val shouldToggleDropdown = pressedDropdown != null && pressedDropdown == releasedDropdown
   957	                    val shouldToggleRememberMe = wasPressingRememberMe && releasedRememberMe
   958	                    val shouldCloseMockSignIn = wasPressingBackChevron && releasedBackChevron
   959	                    val shouldSubmitForgotPassword = wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit && releasedForgotPasswordSubmit
   960	                    val shouldNavigateCreateAvatar = pressedCreateAvatarNav != null &&
   961	                        !createAvatarDragMoved &&
   962	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == releasedCreateAvatarNav
   963	                    val shouldSwipeCreateAvatar = wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   964	                        createAvatarDragMoved &&
   965	                        createAvatarDragDirection != null &&
   966	                        createAvatarDragProgress >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_DRAG_COMMIT_PROGRESS
   967	                    val shouldFocusField = releasedField != null &&
   968	                        !wasTouchingDropdownOptions &&
   969	                        !wasPressingRememberMe &&
   970	                        !wasPressingBackChevron &&
   971	                        !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget &&
   972	                        !wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   973	                        !wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   974	                        pressedDropdown == null &&
   975	                        pressedOption == null &&
   976	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   977	                        (pressedMockField == null || pressedMockField == releasedField)
   978	                    pressedMockField = null
   979	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   980	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   981	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   982	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   983	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   984	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   985	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   986	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   987	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   988	                    this.pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   989	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   990	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   991	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   992	                    super.performClick()
   993	                    if (shouldCloseMockSignIn) {
   994	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   995	                    } else if (shouldSwipeCreateAvatar && createAvatarDragDirection != null) {
   996	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   997	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   998	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(createAvatarDragDirection, createAvatarDragProgress)
   999	                    } else if (shouldNavigateCreateAvatar && pressedCreateAvatarNav != null) {
  1000	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1001	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1002	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(pressedCreateAvatarNav)
  1003	                    } else if (wasDraggingCreateAvatar) {
  1004	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1005	                    } else if (shouldSelectDropdownOption && pressedOption != null) {
  1006	                        setMockDropdownValue(pressedOption)
  1007	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1008	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1009	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1010	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1011	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1012	                    } else if (shouldToggleDropdown && pressedDropdown != null) {
  1013	                        expandedMockDropdown = if (expandedMockDropdown == pressedDropdown) null else pressedDropdown
  1014	                        expandedMockDropdown?.let { prepareMockDropdownScroll(it) }
  1015	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1016	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1017	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1018	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1019	                    } else if (shouldToggleRememberMe) {
  1020	                        rememberMeChecked = !rememberMeChecked
  1021	                        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  1022	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1023	                    } else if (shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget && pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null) {
  1024	                        setMockForgotRecoveryTarget(pressedForgotRecoverySelection)
  1025	                    } else if (shouldSubmitForgotPassword) {
  1026	                        startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  1027	                    } else if (shouldFocusField && releasedField != null) {
  1028	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1029	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1030	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1031	                        focusMockSignInField(releasedField)
  1032	                    } else if (wasTouchingDropdownOptions || wasScrollingDropdown) {
  1033	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1034	                    } else if (!wasPressingRememberMe && !wasPressingBackChevron && !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget) {
  1035	                        if (expandedMockDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1036	                            expandedMockDropdown = null
  1037	                            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1038	                            restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1039	                        }
  1040	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1041	                    } else {
  1042	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1043	                    }
  1044	                    return true
  1045	                }
  1046	                val shouldOpenMockCreateAccount = pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
  1047	                    settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1048	                val shouldOpenMockSignIn = pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1049	                val shouldOpenMockForgotPassword = pressedForgotPasswordPrompt &&
  1050	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1051	                val releasedAvatarProfile = settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
  1052	                val swipeDirection = portraitProfileSwipeDirection()
  1053	                val swipeProgress = portraitProfileDragProgress()
  1054	                val shouldSwipeProfile = isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  1055	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null &&
  1056	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
  1057	                    swipeProgress >= PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_COMMIT_PROGRESS &&
  1058	                    swipeDirection != null
  1059	                val shouldNavigate = !shouldSwipeProfile && !portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
  1060	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null && pressedAvatarProfile == releasedAvatarProfile
  1061	                clickAvatarProfile = pressedAvatarProfile
  1062	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1063	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1064	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1065	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1066	                if (shouldSwipeProfile && swipeDirection != null) {
  1067	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1068	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1069	                    portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1070	                    startPortraitProfileCarousel(swipeDirection, swipeProgress)
  1071	                    true
  1072	                } else if (shouldOpenMockCreateAccount) {
  1073	                    super.performClick()
  1074	                    openMockCreateAccountScreen()
  1075	                    true
  1076	                } else if (shouldOpenMockSignIn) {
  1077	                    super.performClick()
  1078	                    openMockSignInScreen()
  1079	                    true
  1080	                } else if (shouldOpenMockForgotPassword) {
  1081	                    super.performClick()
  1082	                    openMockForgotPasswordScreen()
  1083	                    true
  1084	                } else if (shouldNavigate) {
  1085	                    performClick()
  1086	                    true
  1087	                } else {
  1088	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1089	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1090	                    portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1091	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1092	                    false
  1093	                }
  1094	            }
  1095	            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
  1096	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1097	                clickAvatarProfile = null
  1098	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1099	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1100	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1101	                pressedMockField = null
  1102	                pressedRememberMe = false
  1103	                pressedBackChevron = false
  1104	                pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1105	                activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1106	                mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1107	                mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1108	                lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  1109	                pressedMockDropdown = null
  1110	                pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1111	                pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1112	                pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1113	                activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1114	                mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1115	                mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  1116	                portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
  1117	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1118	                portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1119	                false
  1120	            }
  1121	            else -> pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
  1122	                pressedSignInPrompt ||
  1123	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
  1124	                pressedAvatarProfile != null ||
  1125	                mockSignInStartMillis != null
  1126	        }
  1127	    }
  1128	
  1129	    override fun onCheckIsTextEditor(): Boolean {
  1130	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && !isMockSignInClosing()
  1131	    }
  1132	
  1133	    override fun onCreateInputConnection(outAttrs: EditorInfo): InputConnection? {
  1134	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return null
  1135	        outAttrs.inputType = when (field) {
  1136	            MockSignInField.Password,
  1137	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD
  1138	            MockSignInField.Email -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_EMAIL_ADDRESS
  1139	            MockSignInField.Month,
  1140	            MockSignInField.Day,
  1141	            MockSignInField.Year -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER
  1142	            else -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_NORMAL
  1143	        }
  1144	        outAttrs.imeOptions = if (field == activeMockFields().lastOrNull()) {
  1145	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE
  1146	        } else {
  1147	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_NEXT
  1148	        }
  1149	        val textLength = mockSignInFieldText(field).length
  1150	        outAttrs.initialSelStart = textLength
  1151	        outAttrs.initialSelEnd = textLength
  1152	        return MockSignInInputConnection()
  1153	    }
  1154	
  1155	    override fun onKeyDown(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1156	        if (focusedMockField != null) {
  1157	            return handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode, event) || super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1158	        }
  1159	        return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1160	    }
  1161	
  1162	    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
  1163	    override fun onKeyMultiple(keyCode: Int, repeatCount: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1164	        val characters = event.getCharacters()
  1165	        if (focusedMockField != null && !characters.isNullOrEmpty()) {
  1166	            return appendMockSignInInput(characters)
  1167	        }
  1168	        return super.onKeyMultiple(keyCode, repeatCount, event)
  1169	    }
  1170	
  1171	    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
  1172	        super.performClick()
  1173	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
  1174	        val profile = clickAvatarProfile ?: return false
  1175	        onAvatarSelected?.invoke(profile)
  1176	        clickAvatarProfile = null
  1177	        return true
  1178	    }
  1179	
  1180	    private fun drawSettlingLogo(
  1181	        canvas: Canvas,
  1182	        stageLeft: Float,
  1183	        stageTop: Float,
  1184	        stageScale: Float,
  1185	        progress: Float,
  1186	        alpha: Float
  1187	    ) {
  1188	        val logoEntry = bouncySegmentMs(progress, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
  1189	        val finalProgress = easedSegmentMs(progress, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1190	        val focusShiftY = mockLogoFocusShiftY()
  1191	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1192	        val portraitLogo = Bounds(
  1193	            (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - 600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE) / 2f +
  1194	                PORTRAIT_LOGO_VISIBLE_CENTER_OFFSET_X,
  1195	            portraitLogoTop(),
  1196	            600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE,
  1197	            LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE
  1198	        )
  1199	        val portraitCenteredLogo = Bounds(
  1200	            x = portraitLogo.x + portraitLogo.w / 2f -
  1201	                portraitLogo.w * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE / 2f,
  1202	            y = portraitStackCenter(0.5f) -
  1203	                portraitLogo.h * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE / 2f,
  1204	            w = portraitLogo.w * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE,
  1205	            h = portraitLogo.h * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE
  1206	        )
  1207	        val base = offsetBounds(
  1208	            lerpBounds(Bounds(222f, 150f, 750f, 535f), portraitCenteredLogo, portraitAmount),
  1209	            focusShiftY
  1210	        )
  1211	        val final = offsetBounds(
  1212	            lerpBounds(Bounds(297f, LOGO_FINAL_TOP, 600f, LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT), portraitLogo, portraitAmount),
  1213	            focusShiftY
  1214	        )
  1215	
  1216	        if (logoEntry >= 0.999f) {
  1217	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoCombined, lerpBounds(base, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1218	            return
  1219	        }
  1220	
  1221	        val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(447f, 1037f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1222	        val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(453f, -417f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1223	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoSimple, lerpBounds(simpleEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1224	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoEyes, lerpBounds(eyesEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1225	    }
  1226	
  1227	    private fun drawPortraitProfile(
  1228	        canvas: Canvas,
  1229	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1230	        yOffset: Float,
  1231	        alpha: Float
  1232	    ) {
  1233	        val slideDistance = portraitAvatarSize()
  1234	        val dragDirection = portraitProfileSwipeDirection()
  1235	        val dragProgress = portraitProfileDragProgress()
  1236	        val carouselProgress = portraitProfileCarouselProgress()
  1237	        val carouselDirection = portraitProfileCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  1238	
  1239	        if (portraitProfileSwipeMoved && dragDirection != null && !isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()) {
  1240	            val direction = dragDirection.stageDirection
  1241	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1242	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * (1f - dragProgress), portraitProfileIndex,
  1243	                -direction * dragProgress * slideDistance
  1244	            )
  1245	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1246	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * dragProgress, portraitProfileIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  1247	                direction * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance
  1248	            )
  1249	        } else if (isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()) {
  1250	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1251	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * (1f - carouselProgress), portraitProfileCarouselFromIndex,
  1252	                -carouselDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance
  1253	            )
  1254	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1255	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * carouselProgress, portraitProfileCarouselToIndex,
  1256	                carouselDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance
  1257	            )
  1258	        } else {
  1259	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha, portraitProfileIndex, 0f)
  1260	        }
  1261	    }
  1262	
  1263	    private fun drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1264	        canvas: Canvas,
  1265	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1266	        yOffset: Float,
  1267	        alpha: Float,
  1268	        profileIndex: Int,
  1269	        offsetX: Float
  1270	    ) {
  1271	        val profile = FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[profileIndex].profile
  1272	        val avatarBitmap = when (profile) {
  1273	            CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveAvatar
  1274	            CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinAvatar
  1275	            CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyAvatar
  1276	            CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestAvatar
  1277	        }
  1278	        val nameBitmap = when (profile) {
  1279	            CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveName
  1280	            CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinName
  1281	            CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyName
  1282	            CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestName
  1283	        }
  1284	        val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  1285	        val nameWidth = (if (profile == CinerificProfile.Guest) 200f else 220f) * PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SCALE
  1286	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1287	            canvas,
  1288	            avatarBitmap,
  1289	            Bounds(
  1290	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - avatarSize) / 2f + offsetX,
  1291	                portraitAvatarTop() + yOffset,
  1292	                avatarSize,
  1293	                avatarSize
  1294	            ),
  1295	            stage.left,
  1296	            stage.top,
  1297	            stage.scale,
  1298	            alpha
  1299	        )
  1300	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1301	            canvas,
  1302	            nameBitmap,
  1303	            Bounds(
  1304	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - nameWidth) / 2f + offsetX,
  1305	                portraitProfileNameTop() + yOffset,
  1306	                nameWidth,
  1307	                portraitProfileNameHeight()
  1308	            ),
  1309	            stage.left,
  1310	            stage.top,
  1311	            stage.scale,
  1312	            alpha
  1313	        )
  1314	    }
  1315	
  1316	    private fun drawIntroBackground(canvas: Canvas, solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float) {
  1317	        val standardSignInBackground = standardSignInBackgroundForSize()
  1318	        if (gradientProgress <= 0.001f) {
  1319	            canvas.drawColor(lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress))
  1320	            return
  1321	        }
  1322	
  1323	        val solid = lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress)
  1324	        backgroundPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1325	            0f,
  1326	            0f,
  1327	            0f,
  1328	            height.toFloat(),
  1329	            intArrayOf(
  1330	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_TOP, gradientProgress),
  1331	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_CENTER, gradientProgress),
  1332	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_BOTTOM, gradientProgress)
  1333	            ),
  1334	            null,
  1335	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1336	        )
  1337	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1338	        backgroundPaint.shader = null
  1339	
  1340	        if (standardSignInBackground != null) {
  1341	            drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas, gradientProgress)
  1342	            drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas, standardSignInBackground, gradientProgress)
  1343	        }
  1344	    }
  1345	
  1346	    private fun drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas: Canvas, alpha: Float) {
  1347	        backgroundPaint.color = COLOR_FRAME_1
  1348	        backgroundPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1349	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1350	        backgroundPaint.alpha = 255
  1351	    }
  1352	
  1353	    private fun drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas: Canvas, bitmap: Bitmap, alpha: Float) {
  1354	        tempRect.set(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat())
  1355	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * STANDARD_BACKGROUND_SETTLED_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1356	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1357	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1358	    }
  1359	
  1360	    private fun drawFigmaBitmap(
  1361	        canvas: Canvas,
  1362	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  1363	        bounds: Bounds,
  1364	        stageLeft: Float,
  1365	        stageTop: Float,
  1366	        stageScale: Float,
  1367	        alpha: Float
  1368	    ) {
  1369	        tempRect.set(
  1370	            stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale,
  1371	            stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale,
  1372	            stageLeft + (bounds.x + bounds.w) * stageScale,
  1373	            stageTop + (bounds.y + bounds.h) * stageScale
  1374	        )
  1375	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1376	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1377	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1378	    }
  1379	
  1380	    private fun drawAccountPrompt(
  1381	        canvas: Canvas,
  1382	        stageLeft: Float,
  1383	        stageTop: Float,
  1384	        stageScale: Float,
  1385	        yOffset: Float,
  1386	        alpha: Float
  1387	    ) {
  1388	        if (usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout(activeMockFlow) ||
  1389	            usesPortraitStackedAuthLayout(outgoingMockFlow)
  1390	        ) {
  1391	            return
  1392	        }
  1393	        val isLandscape = width > height
  1394	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1395	        val textSize = lerpFloat(
  1396	            signInAccountPromptTextSize(),
  1397	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE,
  1398	            portraitAmount
  1399	        ) * stageScale
  1400	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1401	        accountPromptPaint.textSize = textSize
  1402	        formTitlePaint.textSize = textSize
  1403	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1404	        formTitlePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1405	
  1406	        val accountPromptShiftY = if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1407	        val standardCenterY = stageTop +
  1408	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + accountPromptShiftY + yOffset) * stageScale
  1409	        val secondaryMetrics = accountPromptPaint.fontMetrics
  1410	        val primaryMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1411	        fun promptCenterY(portraitCenterY: Float): Float = lerpFloat(
  1412	            standardCenterY,
  1413	            stageTop + (portraitCenterY + yOffset) * stageScale,
  1414	            portraitAmount
  1415	        )
  1416	        val createCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptCreateCenterY())
  1417	        val signInCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptSignInCenterY())
  1418	        val forgotCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptForgotCenterY())
  1419	        val createBaselineY = createCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1420	        val signInBaselineY = signInCenterY - (primaryMetrics.ascent + primaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1421	        val forgotBaselineY = forgotCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1422	        val portraitCenterX = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH / 2f
  1423	        if (
  1424	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  1425	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
  1426	        ) {
  1427	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1428	            canvas.drawText(
  1429	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
  1430	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptCreateCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1431	                createBaselineY,
  1432	                accountPromptPaint
  1433	            )
  1434	        }
  1435	        if (
  1436	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn &&
  1437	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  1438	        ) {
  1439	            formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1440	            canvas.drawText(
  1441	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1442	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X, portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1443	                signInBaselineY,
  1444	                formTitlePaint
  1445	            )
  1446	        }
  1447	        if (
  1448	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  1449	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  1450	        ) {
  1451	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1452	            canvas.drawText(
  1453	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
  1454	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptForgotCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1455	                forgotBaselineY,
  1456	                accountPromptPaint
  1457	            )
  1458	        }
  1459	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = 255
  1460	        accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1461	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1462	    }

  1464	    private fun drawMockSignInForm(
  1465	        canvas: Canvas,
  1466	        stageLeft: Float,
  1467	        stageTop: Float,
  1468	        stageScale: Float,
  1469	        progress: Float,
  1470	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1471	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean = true
  1472	    ) {
  1473	        if (updateLabelAnimation) {
  1474	            updateMockFormLabelAnimation()
  1475	        }
  1476	
  1477	        val alpha = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1478	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1479	            drawMockCreateAccountForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1480	            return
  1481	        }
  1482	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  1483	            drawMockForgotPasswordForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1484	            return
  1485	        }
  1486	
  1487	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha)
  1488	        val fieldFillAlpha = (alpha * 26f).roundToInt()
  1489	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()
  1490	        val baseStage = StageMetrics(stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  1491	        val usernameY = mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset
  1492	        val passwordY = usernameY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_GAP
  1493	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1494	        val contentStage = mockSignInContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  1495	        val contentLeft = contentStage.left
  1496	        val contentTop = contentStage.top
  1497	        val contentScale = contentStage.scale
  1498	        val fieldX = mockFormX()
  1499	
  1500	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fieldFillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1501	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(fieldStrokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1502	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * contentScale
  1503	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * contentScale
  1504	
  1505	        val rememberMeY = passwordY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + focusShiftY
  1506	        drawMockSignInTitle(canvas, usernameY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1507	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Username, fieldX, usernameY + focusShiftY, contentLeft, contentTop, contentScale, alpha)
  1508	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Password, fieldX, passwordY + focusShiftY, contentLeft, contentTop, contentScale, alpha)
  1509	        drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  1510	            canvas,
  1511	            fieldX,
  1512	            rememberMeY,
  1513	            contentLeft,
  1514	            contentTop,
  1515	            contentScale,
  1516	            alpha
  1517	        )
  1518	        drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1519	            canvas,
  1520	            contentLeft,
  1521	            contentTop,
  1522	            contentScale,
  1523	            alpha,
  1524	            yOffset,
  1525	            focusShiftY
  1526	        )
  1527	
  1528	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
  1529	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
  1530	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1531	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
  1532	    }

  1701	            contentTop,
  1702	            contentScale,
  1703	            alpha,
  1704	            formWidth
  1705	        )
  1706	        drawMockSignInField(
  1707	            canvas,
  1708	            MockSignInField.Email,
  1709	            fieldX,
  1710	            emailY,
  1711	            contentLeft,
  1712	            contentTop,
  1713	            contentScale,
  1714	            alpha,
  1715	            formWidth
  1716	        )
  1717	        drawMockForgotPasswordButton(
  1718	            canvas,
  1719	            fieldX,
  1720	            buttonY,
  1721	            contentLeft,
  1722	            contentTop,
  1723	            contentScale,
  1724	            alpha,
  1725	            formWidth
  1726	        )
  1727	        drawMockFormParagraph(
  1728	            canvas,
  1729	            mockForgotPasswordHelpText(),
  1730	            fieldX + formWidth / 2f,
  1731	            helpTopY,
  1732	            formWidth,
  1733	            contentLeft,
  1734	            contentTop,
  1735	            contentScale,
  1736	            alpha,
  1737	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_TEXT_SIZE,
  1738	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_LINE_HEIGHT
  1739	        )
  1740	        drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1741	            canvas,
  1742	            contentLeft,
  1743	            contentTop,
  1744	            contentScale,
  1745	            alpha,
  1746	            yOffset,
  1747	            focusShiftY
  1748	        )
  1749	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  1750	            canvas.restore()
  1751	        }
  1752	
  1753	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
  1754	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
  1755	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1756	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
  1757	    }
  1758	
  1759	    private fun drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1760	        canvas: Canvas,
  1761	        stageLeft: Float,
  1762	        stageTop: Float,
  1763	        stageScale: Float,
  1764	        alpha: Float,
  1765	        yOffset: Float,
  1766	        focusShiftY: Float
  1767	    ) {
  1768	        val dimProgress = mockInputDimProgress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1769	        if (dimProgress <= 0.01f) return
  1770	
  1771	        inputDimPaint.alpha = (dimProgress * MOCK_INPUT_DIM_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1772	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), inputDimPaint)
  1773	        inputDimPaint.alpha = 255
  1774	
  1775	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 26f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1776	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 185f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1777	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1778	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1779	
  1780	        val activeDropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: mockInputDimActiveDropdown
  1781	        if (activeDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1782	            drawMockDropdownControl(
  1783	                canvas,
  1784	                activeDropdown,
  1785	                mockDropdownX(activeDropdown),
  1786	                mockDropdownY(activeDropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1787	                stageLeft,
  1788	                stageTop,
  1789	                stageScale,
  1790	                alpha,
  1791	                mockDropdownWidth(activeDropdown)
  1792	            )
  1793	            if (expandedMockDropdown == activeDropdown) {
  1794	                drawMockDropdownOptions(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1795	            }
  1796	            return
  1797	        }
  1798	
  1799	        val activeField = focusedMockField ?: mockInputDimActiveField ?: return
  1800	        drawMockSignInField(
  1801	            canvas,
  1802	            activeField,
  1803	            mockActiveFieldX(activeField),
  1804	            mockActiveFieldY(activeField, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1805	            stageLeft,
  1806	            stageTop,
  1807	            stageScale,
  1808	            alpha,
  1809	            mockActiveFieldWidth(activeField)
  1810	        )
  1811	    }

  1813	    private fun drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
  1814	        canvas: Canvas,
  1815	        flow: MockAccountFlow,
  1816	        stageLeft: Float,
  1817	        stageTop: Float,
  1818	        stageScale: Float,
  1819	        progress: Float,
  1820	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1821	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean
  1822	    ) {
  1823	        val currentFlow = activeMockFlow
  1824	        activeMockFlow = flow
  1825	        try {
  1826	            drawMockSignInForm(
  1827	                canvas,
  1828	                stageLeft,
  1829	                stageTop,
  1830	                stageScale,
  1831	                progress,
  1832	                formFocusMotion,
  1833	                updateLabelAnimation
  1834	            )
  1835	        } finally {
  1836	            activeMockFlow = currentFlow
  1837	        }
  1838	    }

  1840	    private fun drawMockSignInTitle(
  1841	        canvas: Canvas,
  1842	        fieldTopY: Float,
  1843	        stageLeft: Float,
  1844	        stageTop: Float,
  1845	        stageScale: Float,
  1846	        alpha: Float
  1847	    ) {
  1848	        val motion = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1849	        val portrait = isPortraitIntroLayout()
  1850	        val portraitTextSize = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE * stageScale
  1851	        val sourceTextSize = if (portrait) portraitTextSize else signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
  1852	        val targetTextSize = if (portrait) portraitTextSize else MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1853	        val sourceCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1854	        val targetCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1855	        val sourceCenterY = stageTop + (if (portrait) {
  1856	            portraitPromptSignInCenterY()
  1857	        } else {
  1858	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()
  1859	        }) * stageScale
  1860	        formTitlePaint.textSize = sourceTextSize
  1861	        val sourceMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1862	        val sourceBaselineY = sourceCenterY - (sourceMetrics.ascent + sourceMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1863	        val targetGap = if (portrait) MOCK_FORM_PORTRAIT_TITLE_BASELINE_GAP else MOCK_FORM_TITLE_BASELINE_GAP
  1864	        val targetBaselineY = stageTop + (fieldTopY - targetGap) * stageScale
  1865	
  1866	        formTitlePaint.textSize = lerpFloat(sourceTextSize, targetTextSize, motion)
  1867	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1868	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1869	        canvas.drawText(
  1870	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1871	            lerpFloat(sourceCenterX, targetCenterX, motion),
  1872	            lerpFloat(sourceBaselineY, targetBaselineY, motion),
  1873	            formTitlePaint
  1874	        )
  1875	    }

  1958	
  1959	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButton(
  1960	        canvas: Canvas,
  1961	        x: Float,
  1962	        y: Float,
  1963	        stageLeft: Float,
  1964	        stageTop: Float,
  1965	        stageScale: Float,
  1966	        alpha: Float,
  1967	        buttonWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  1968	    ) {
  1969	        tempRect.set(
  1970	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  1971	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  1972	            stageLeft + (x + buttonWidth) * stageScale,
  1973	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  1974	        )
  1975	        val radius = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_RADIUS * stageScale
  1976	        val submissionState = forgotPasswordSubmissionState
  1977	        val enabled = mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  1978	        val pressed = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit && enabled
  1979	        val fillAlpha = when {
  1980	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 86f
  1981	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 72f
  1982	            pressed -> 92f
  1983	            enabled -> 68f
  1984	            else -> 18f
  1985	        }
  1986	        val strokeAlpha = when {
  1987	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 245f
  1988	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 228f
  1989	            pressed -> 238f
  1990	            enabled -> 210f
  1991	            else -> 118f
  1992	        }
  1993	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  1994	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * fillAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1995	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * strokeAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1996	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1997	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1998	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1999	        if (submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  2000	            drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(canvas, tempRect, radius, alpha)
  2001	        }
  2002	
  2003	        formTitlePaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2004	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2005	        val metrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  2006	        val baselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2007	        val maxTextWidth = buttonWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  2008	        val showCheck = submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  2009	        val checkSize = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_SIZE * stageScale else 0f
  2010	        val checkGap = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_GAP * stageScale else 0f
  2011	        val textMaxWidth = (maxTextWidth - checkSize - checkGap).coerceAtLeast(maxTextWidth * 0.5f)
  2012	        val buttonText = trailingFittingText(
  2013	            mockForgotPasswordButtonText(),
  2014	            if (showCheck) textMaxWidth else maxTextWidth,
  2015	            formTitlePaint
  2016	        )
  2017	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * when {
  2018	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 226f
  2019	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 255f
  2020	            enabled -> 255f
  2021	            else -> 138f
  2022	        }).roundToInt()
  2023	        formTitlePaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2024	        val textX = if (showCheck) {
  2025	            val textWidth = formTitlePaint.measureText(buttonText)
  2026	            val groupWidth = checkSize + checkGap + textWidth
  2027	            val groupLeft = tempRect.centerX() - groupWidth / 2f
  2028	            val checkLeft = groupLeft
  2029	            val checkCenterY = tempRect.centerY()
  2030	            backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2031	            backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 2.1f * stageScale
  2032	            canvas.drawLine(
  2033	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.18f,
  2034	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.03f,
  2035	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  2036	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  2037	                backChevronPaint
  2038	            )
  2039	            canvas.drawLine(
  2040	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  2041	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  2042	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.84f,
  2043	                checkCenterY - checkSize * 0.28f,
  2044	                backChevronPaint
  2045	            )
  2046	            groupLeft + checkSize + checkGap + textWidth / 2f
  2047	        } else {
  2048	            tempRect.centerX()
  2049	        }
  2050	        canvas.drawText(
  2051	            buttonText,
  2052	            textX,
  2053	            baselineY,
  2054	            formTitlePaint
  2055	        )
  2056	    }
  2057	
  2058	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(
  2059	        canvas: Canvas,
  2060	        buttonRect: RectF,
  2061	        radius: Float,
  2062	        alpha: Float
  2063	    ) {
  2064	        val progress = mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress()
  2065	        val sweepWidth = buttonRect.width() * 0.44f
  2066	        val sweepCenterX = buttonRect.left + buttonRect.width() * (progress * 1.35f - 0.18f)
  2067	        val sweepLeft = sweepCenterX - sweepWidth / 2f
  2068	        val sweepRight = sweepCenterX + sweepWidth / 2f
  2069	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  2070	            sweepLeft,
  2071	            buttonRect.top,
  2072	            sweepRight,
  2073	            buttonRect.top,
  2074	            intArrayOf(
  2075	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255),
  2076	                Color.argb((alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 54f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255),
  2077	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255)
  2078	            ),
  2079	            floatArrayOf(0f, 0.5f, 1f),
  2080	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  2081	        )
  2082	        canvas.save()
  2083	        tempPath.reset()
  2084	        tempPath.addRoundRect(buttonRect, radius, radius, Path.Direction.CW)
  2085	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  2086	        canvas.drawRect(sweepLeft, buttonRect.top, sweepRight, buttonRect.bottom, formFieldFillPaint)
  2087	        canvas.restore()
  2088	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  2089	    }

  2091	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoverySelector(
  2092	        canvas: Canvas,

  2132	            rowTop,
  2133	            layout.boxWidth,
  2134	            stageScale,
  2135	            alpha
  2136	        )
  2137	        drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  2138	            canvas,
  2139	            MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password,
  2140	            layout.passwordBoxX,
  2141	            rowTop,
  2142	            layout.boxWidth,
  2143	            stageScale,
  2144	            alpha
  2145	        )

  2148	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  2149	        canvas: Canvas,
  2150	        target: MockForgotRecoveryTarget,
  2151	        left: Float,
  2152	        top: Float,
  2153	        boxWidth: Float,
  2154	        stageScale: Float,
  2155	        alpha: Float
  2156	    ) {
  2157	        val right = left + boxWidth
  2158	        val bottom = top + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT * stageScale
  2159	        tempRect.set(left, top, right, bottom)
  2160	        val selected = forgotRecoveryTarget == target
  2161	        val pressed = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == target
  2162	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * when {
  2163	            pressed -> 82f
  2164	            selected -> 64f
  2165	            else -> 14f
  2166	        }).roundToInt()
  2167	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * when {
  2168	            pressed -> 245f
  2169	            selected -> 235f
  2170	            else -> 142f
  2171	        }).roundToInt()
  2172	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2173	
  2174	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2175	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2176	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2177	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2178	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2179	
  2180	        formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_OPTION_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2181	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (selected || pressed) 255f else 178f).roundToInt()
  2182	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2183	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2184	        canvas.drawText(
  2185	            target.displayText,
  2186	            tempRect.centerX(),
  2187	            tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  2188	            formInputPaint
  2189	        )
  2190	    }

  2192	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordBodyParagraph(
  2193	        canvas: Canvas,
  2194	        text: String,
  2195	        centerX: Float,
  2196	        topY: Float,
  2197	        maxWidth: Float,
  2198	        stageLeft: Float,
  2199	        stageTop: Float,
  2200	        stageScale: Float,
  2201	        alpha: Float
  2202	    ): Float {
  2203	        val paragraphs = text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  2204	        val leadBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2205	            canvas,
  2206	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  2207	            centerX,
  2208	            topY,
  2209	            maxWidth,
  2210	            stageLeft,
  2211	            stageTop,
  2212	            stageScale,
  2213	            alpha,
  2214	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  2215	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT
  2216	        )
  2217	        val middleText = paragraphs.getOrNull(1) ?: return leadBottomY
  2218	        val middleBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2219	            canvas,
  2220	            middleText,
  2221	            centerX,
  2222	            leadBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2223	            maxWidth,
  2224	            stageLeft,
  2225	            stageTop,
  2226	            stageScale,
  2227	            alpha,
  2228	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2229	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2230	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2231	        )
  2232	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  2233	        if (bottomText.isEmpty()) return middleBottomY
  2234	        return drawMockFormParagraph(
  2235	            canvas,
  2236	            bottomText,
  2237	            centerX,
  2238	            middleBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2239	            maxWidth,
  2240	            stageLeft,
  2241	            stageTop,
  2242	            stageScale,
  2243	            alpha,
  2244	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2245	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2246	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2247	        )
  2248	    }
  2249	
  2250	    private fun drawMockFormParagraph(
  2251	        canvas: Canvas,
  2252	        text: String,
  2253	        centerX: Float,
  2254	        topY: Float,
  2255	        maxWidth: Float,
  2256	        stageLeft: Float,
  2257	        stageTop: Float,
  2258	        stageScale: Float,
  2259	        alpha: Float,
  2260	        textSize: Float,
  2261	        lineHeight: Float,
  2262	        textAlign: Paint.Align = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2263	    ): Float {
  2264	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize * stageScale
  2265	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 218f).roundToInt()
  2266	        formInputPaint.textAlign = textAlign
  2267	
  2268	        val lines = wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  2269	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2270	        val x = when (textAlign) {
  2271	            Paint.Align.LEFT -> stageLeft + (centerX - maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2272	            Paint.Align.RIGHT -> stageLeft + (centerX + maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2273	            Paint.Align.CENTER -> stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2274	        }
  2275	        var baselineY = stageTop + topY * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2276	        lines.forEach { line ->
  2277	            canvas.drawText(line, x, baselineY, formInputPaint)
  2278	            baselineY += lineHeight * stageScale
  2279	        }
  2280	        return topY + lines.size * lineHeight
  2281	    }
