  2283	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  2284	        canvas: Canvas,
  2285	        columnX: Float,
  2286	        firstFieldY: Float,
  2287	        stageLeft: Float,
  2288	        stageTop: Float,
  2289	        stageScale: Float,
  2290	        alpha: Float
  2291	    ) {
  2292	        val avatarScale = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  2293	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * avatarScale
  2294	        val labelBaselineFromTop = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LABEL_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * avatarScale
  2295	        val avatarTopGap = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_TOP_GAP * avatarScale
  2296	        val centerX = stageLeft + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * stageScale
  2297	        val baselineY = stageTop + (firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop) * stageScale
  2298	        formLabelPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * avatarScale * stageScale
  2299	        formLabelPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2300	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2301	        canvas.drawText(
  2302	            MOCK_FORM_AVATAR_TEXT,
  2303	            centerX,
  2304	            baselineY,
  2305	            formLabelPaint
  2306	        )
  2307	
  2308	        val avatarLeftX = columnX + (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - scaledAvatarSize) / 2f
  2309	        val avatarTopY = firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop + avatarTopGap
  2310	        val avatarBounds = Bounds(
  2311	            avatarLeftX,
  2312	            avatarTopY,
  2313	            scaledAvatarSize,
  2314	            scaledAvatarSize
  2315	        )
  2316	        val carouselProgress = mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  2317	        val slideDirection = mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  2318	        val slideDistance = mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize)
  2319	        val dragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  2320	        val dragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance, stageScale)
  2321	
  2322	        drawMockCreateAvatarBubbleShell(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  2323	
  2324	        canvas.save()
  2325	        clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  2326	        if (
  2327	            activeMockCreateAvatarDrag &&
  2328	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  2329	            dragDirection != null &&
  2330	            !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  2331	        ) {
  2332	            val dragSlideDirection = dragDirection.stageDirection
  2333	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2334	                canvas,
  2335	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  2336	                avatarBounds,
  2337	                -dragSlideDirection * dragProgress * slideDistance,
  2338	                stageLeft,
  2339	                stageTop,
  2340	                stageScale,
  2341	                alpha
  2342	            )
  2343	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2344	                canvas,
  2345	                mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  2346	                avatarBounds,
  2347	                dragSlideDirection * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance,
  2348	                stageLeft,
  2349	                stageTop,
  2350	                stageScale,
  2351	                alpha
  2352	            )
  2353	        } else if (isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) {
  2354	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2355	                canvas,
  2356	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex,
  2357	                avatarBounds,
  2358	                -slideDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance,
  2359	                stageLeft,
  2360	                stageTop,
  2361	                stageScale,
  2362	                alpha
  2363	            )
  2364	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2365	                canvas,
  2366	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex,
  2367	                avatarBounds,
  2368	                slideDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance,
  2369	                stageLeft,
  2370	                stageTop,
  2371	                stageScale,
  2372	                alpha
  2373	            )
  2374	        } else {
  2375	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2376	                canvas,
  2377	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  2378	                avatarBounds,
  2379	                0f,
  2380	                stageLeft,
  2381	                stageTop,
  2382	                stageScale,
  2383	                alpha
  2384	            )
  2385	        }
  2386	        canvas.restore()
  2387	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2388	            canvas,
  2389	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  2390	            avatarBounds,
  2391	            stageLeft,
  2392	            stageTop,
  2393	            stageScale,
  2394	            alpha
  2395	        )
  2396	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2397	            canvas,
  2398	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2399	            avatarBounds,
  2400	            stageLeft,
  2401	            stageTop,
  2402	            stageScale,
  2403	            alpha
  2404	        )
  2405	    }

  2447	    private fun clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(
  2448	        canvas: Canvas,
  2449	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2450	        stageLeft: Float,
  2451	        stageTop: Float,
  2452	        stageScale: Float
  2453	    ) {
  2454	        tempPath.reset()
  2455	        tempPath.addCircle(
  2456	            stageLeft + (avatarBounds.x + avatarBounds.w / 2f) * stageScale,
  2457	            stageTop + (avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h / 2f) * stageScale,
  2458	            mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds, stageScale),
  2459	            Path.Direction.CW
  2460	        )
  2461	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  2462	    }

  2464	    private fun mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds: Bounds, stageScale: Float): Float {
  2465	        return (avatarBounds.w / 2f - avatarBounds.w * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO) * stageScale
  2466	    }

  2468	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2469	        canvas: Canvas,
  2470	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2471	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2472	        stageLeft: Float,
  2473	        stageTop: Float,
  2474	        stageScale: Float,
  2475	        alpha: Float
  2476	    ) {
  2477	        val centerX = stageLeft + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2478	        val centerY = stageTop + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2479	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2480	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale / 2f
  2481	        val pressedAlpha = if (pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == direction) 0.62f else 0.9f
  2482	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * pressedAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2483	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2484	
  2485	        if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  2486	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX - halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2487	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY, centerX + halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2488	        } else {
  2489	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX + halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2490	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY, centerX - halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2491	        }
  2492	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2493	    }

  2495	    private fun drawMockDropdownControl(
  2496	        canvas: Canvas,
  2497	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2498	        x: Float,
  2499	        y: Float,
  2500	        stageLeft: Float,
  2501	        stageTop: Float,
  2502	        stageScale: Float,
  2503	        alpha: Float,
  2504	        dropdownWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2505	    ) {
  2506	        tempRect.set(
  2507	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2508	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2509	            stageLeft + (x + dropdownWidth) * stageScale,
  2510	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2511	        )
  2512	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2513	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2514	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2515	
  2516	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2517	        formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2518	        formInputPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2519	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2520	        val textInset = mockDropdownTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2521	        val chevronInset = mockDropdownChevronInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2522	        val displayText = mockDropdownDisplayText(dropdown)
  2523	        val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(displayText)
  2524	            ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  2525	        val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2526	            (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  2527	        } else {
  2528	            0f
  2529	        }
  2530	        val maxTextWidth = (dropdownWidth * stageScale - textInset - iconLaneWidth - chevronInset * 2f)
  2531	            .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2532	        if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2533	            drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2534	                canvas = canvas,
  2535	                bitmap = membershipIcon,
  2536	                left = tempRect.left + textInset,
  2537	                centerY = tempRect.centerY(),
  2538	                iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  2539	                alpha = textAlpha
  2540	            )
  2541	        }
  2542	        canvas.drawText(
  2543	            trailingFittingText(displayText, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  2544	            tempRect.left + textInset + iconLaneWidth,
  2545	            tempRect.centerY() - (formInputPaint.fontMetrics.ascent + formInputPaint.fontMetrics.descent) / 2f,
  2546	            formInputPaint
  2547	        )
  2548	
  2549	        val centerX = tempRect.right - chevronInset
  2550	        val centerY = tempRect.centerY() + 2f * stageScale
  2551	        val halfSize = mockDropdownChevronSize(dropdown) * stageScale / 2f
  2552	        backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2553	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 1.65f * stageScale
  2554	        canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  2555	        canvas.drawLine(centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, centerX + halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  2556	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2557	    }

  2559	    private fun drawMockDropdownOptions(
  2560	        canvas: Canvas,
  2561	        stageLeft: Float,
  2562	        stageTop: Float,
  2563	        stageScale: Float,
  2564	        alpha: Float
  2565	    ) {
  2566	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return
  2567	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return
  2568	
  2569	        val x = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  2570	        val y = mockDropdownOptionsY(
  2571	            dropdown,
  2572	            MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha),
  2573	            mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2574	        )
  2575	        val left = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  2576	        val top = stageTop + y * stageScale
  2577	        val right = stageLeft + (x + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stageScale
  2578	        val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stageScale
  2579	        val optionAlpha = 255
  2580	        val optionTextInset = mockDropdownOptionTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2581	        val scrollbarReservedWidth = if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2582	            (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stageScale
  2583	        } else {
  2584	            0f
  2585	        }
  2586	
  2587	        val visibleOptions = mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown)
  2588	        visibleOptions.forEachIndexed { index, option ->
  2589	            val optionTop = top + index * optionHeight
  2590	            val optionBottom = optionTop + optionHeight
  2591	            val fillColor = if (option == mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown)) {
  2592	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 76, 12, 94)
  2593	            } else {
  2594	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 14, 2, 18)
  2595	            }
  2596	            tempRect.set(left, optionTop, right, optionBottom)
  2597	            formFieldFillPaint.color = fillColor
  2598	            formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(optionAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2599	            formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.2f * stageScale
  2600	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldFillPaint)
  2601	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2602	
  2603	            formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownOptionTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2604	            formInputPaint.alpha = optionAlpha
  2605	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2606	            val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2607	            val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(option)
  2608	                ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  2609	            val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2610	                (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_OPTION_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  2611	            } else {
  2612	                0f
  2613	            }
  2614	            if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2615	                drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2616	                    canvas = canvas,
  2617	                    bitmap = membershipIcon,
  2618	                    left = left + optionTextInset,
  2619	                    centerY = (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f,
  2620	                    iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_OPTION_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  2621	                    alpha = optionAlpha
  2622	                )
  2623	            }
  2624	            val maxTextWidth = (right - left - optionTextInset - iconLaneWidth - scrollbarReservedWidth)
  2625	                .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2626	            canvas.drawText(
  2627	                trailingFittingText(option, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  2628	                left + optionTextInset + iconLaneWidth,
  2629	                (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  2630	                formInputPaint
  2631	            )
  2632	        }
  2633	
  2634	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2635	            drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  2636	                canvas,
  2637	                dropdown,
  2638	                top,
  2639	                right,
  2640	                optionHeight * visibleOptions.size,
  2641	                stageScale,
  2642	                optionAlpha
  2643	            )
  2644	        }
  2645	    }
  2646	
  2647	    private fun drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2648	        canvas: Canvas,
  2649	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  2650	        left: Float,
  2651	        centerY: Float,
  2652	        iconSize: Float,
  2653	        alpha: Int
  2654	    ) {
  2655	        val bitmapAspect = bitmap.width.toFloat() / bitmap.height.toFloat()
  2656	        val width: Float
  2657	        val height: Float
  2658	        if (bitmapAspect >= 1f) {
  2659	            width = iconSize
  2660	            height = iconSize / bitmapAspect
  2661	        } else {
  2662	            width = iconSize * bitmapAspect
  2663	            height = iconSize
  2664	        }
  2665	        val top = centerY - height / 2f
  2666	        tempIconRect.set(left, top, left + width, top + height)
  2667	        imagePaint.alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0, 255)
  2668	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempIconRect, imagePaint)
  2669	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  2670	    }
  2671	
  2672	    private fun drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  2673	        canvas: Canvas,
  2674	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2675	        top: Float,
  2676	        right: Float,
  2677	        dropdownHeight: Float,
  2678	        stageScale: Float,
  2679	        alpha: Int
  2680	    ) {
  2681	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  2682	        val visibleCount = mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  2683	        val maxOffset = mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown)
  2684	        if (options.isEmpty() || maxOffset <= 0) return
  2685	
  2686	        val trackRight = right - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * stageScale
  2687	        val trackLeft = trackRight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale
  2688	        val trackTop = top + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  2689	        val trackBottom = top + dropdownHeight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  2690	        val trackHeight = (trackBottom - trackTop).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2691	        val thumbHeight = maxOf(14f * stageScale, trackHeight * visibleCount / options.size)
  2692	            .coerceAtMost(trackHeight)
  2693	        val thumbProgress = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown) / maxOffset.toFloat()
  2694	        val thumbTop = trackTop + (trackHeight - thumbHeight) * thumbProgress
  2695	        val radius = MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2696	
  2697	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, trackTop, trackRight, trackBottom)
  2698	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.28f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2699	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2700	
  2701	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, thumbTop, trackRight, thumbTop + thumbHeight)
  2702	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.78f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2703	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2704	    }

  2706	    private fun drawMockSignInField(
  2707	        canvas: Canvas,
  2708	        field: MockSignInField,
  2709	        x: Float,
  2710	        y: Float,
  2711	        stageLeft: Float,
  2712	        stageTop: Float,
  2713	        stageScale: Float,
  2714	        alpha: Float,
  2715	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2716	    ) {
  2717	        tempRect.set(
  2718	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2719	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2720	            stageLeft + (x + fieldWidth) * stageScale,
  2721	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2722	        )
  2723	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2724	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2725	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2726	
  2727	        val label = mockSignInFieldLabel(field)
  2728	        val labelProgress = mockSignInFieldLabelProgress(field)
  2729	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2730	        formLabelPaint.textSize = lerpFloat(
  2731	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE,
  2732	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * MOCK_FORM_FLOATING_LABEL_SCALE,
  2733	            labelProgress
  2734	        ) * stageScale
  2735	        formLabelPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2736	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2737	        val metrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
  2738	        val placeholderBaselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2739	        val floatingBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_TOP_INSET_Y * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2740	        val labelX = lerpFloat(
  2741	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale,
  2742	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_INSET_X * stageScale,
  2743	            labelProgress
  2744	        )
  2745	        val labelBaselineY = lerpFloat(placeholderBaselineY, floatingBaselineY, labelProgress)
  2746	        canvas.drawText(
  2747	            label,
  2748	            labelX,
  2749	            labelBaselineY,
  2750	            formLabelPaint
  2751	        )
  2752	
  2753	        val fieldText = mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field)
  2754	        val shouldDrawInput = fieldText.isNotEmpty() || focusedMockField == field
  2755	        if (shouldDrawInput) {
  2756	            val inputAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * labelProgress * 255f).roundToInt()
  2757	            formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2758	            formInputPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  2759	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2760	            formCaretPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  2761	
  2762	            val inputX = tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale
  2763	            val inputBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_INPUT_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * stageScale
  2764	            val maxInputWidth = fieldWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  2765	            val visibleText = trailingFittingText(fieldText, maxInputWidth, formInputPaint)
  2766	            canvas.drawText(visibleText, inputX, inputBaselineY, formInputPaint)
  2767	
  2768	            if (focusedMockField == field) {
  2769	                val caretX = inputX + formInputPaint.measureText(visibleText) + 2f * stageScale
  2770	                val inputMetrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2771	                canvas.drawLine(
  2772	                    caretX,
  2773	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.ascent,
  2774	                    caretX,
  2775	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.descent,
  2776	                    formCaretPaint
  2777	                )
  2778	            }
  2779	        }
  2780	    }

  2782	    private fun drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  2783	        canvas: Canvas,
  2784	        x: Float,
  2785	        y: Float,
  2786	        stageLeft: Float,
  2787	        stageTop: Float,
  2788	        stageScale: Float,
  2789	        alpha: Float
  2790	    ) {
  2791	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2792	        val boxLeft = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  2793	        val boxTop = stageTop + y * stageScale
  2794	        val boxRight = boxLeft + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  2795	        val boxBottom = boxTop + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  2796	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked || pressedRememberMe) 46f else 18f).roundToInt()
  2797	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked) 220f else 168f).roundToInt()
  2798	
  2799	        rememberMeBoxFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2800	        rememberMeBoxStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2801	        rememberMeBoxStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.7f * stageScale
  2802	        tempRect.set(boxLeft, boxTop, boxRight, boxBottom)
  2803	        val radius = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_RADIUS * stageScale
  2804	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxFillPaint)
  2805	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxStrokePaint)
  2806	
  2807	        if (rememberMeChecked) {
  2808	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2809	            rememberMeCheckPaint.strokeWidth = 2.45f * stageScale
  2810	            canvas.drawLine(
  2811	                boxLeft + 5.3f * stageScale,
  2812	                boxTop + 11.8f * stageScale,
  2813	                boxLeft + 9.1f * stageScale,
  2814	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  2815	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  2816	            )
  2817	            canvas.drawLine(
  2818	                boxLeft + 9.1f * stageScale,
  2819	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  2820	                boxLeft + 17.2f * stageScale,
  2821	                boxTop + 6.9f * stageScale,
  2822	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  2823	            )
  2824	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = 255
  2825	        }
  2826	
  2827	        rememberMeTextPaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2828	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2829	        rememberMeTextPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2830	        val metrics = rememberMeTextPaint.fontMetrics
  2831	        val baselineY = (boxTop + boxBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2832	        canvas.drawText(
  2833	            MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT,
  2834	            boxRight + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_LABEL_GAP * stageScale,
  2835	            baselineY,
  2836	            rememberMeTextPaint
  2837	        )
  2838	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = 255
  2839	    }

  2841	    private fun drawBackChevron(
  2842	        canvas: Canvas,
  2843	        centerX: Float,
  2844	        topY: Float,
  2845	        stageLeft: Float,
  2846	        stageTop: Float,
  2847	        stageScale: Float,
  2848	        alpha: Float
  2849	    ) {
  2850	        val chevronAlpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (pressedBackChevron) 0.68f else 0.92f
  2851	        val center = stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2852	        val top = stageTop + topY * stageScale
  2853	        val bottom = top + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale
  2854	        val halfWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2855	
  2856	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (chevronAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2857	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2858	        canvas.drawLine(center - halfWidth, top, center, bottom, backChevronPaint)
  2859	        canvas.drawLine(center, bottom, center + halfWidth, top, backChevronPaint)
  2860	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2861	    }

  2863	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {
  2864	        return BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, resId, bitmapOptions)
  2865	    }
  2866	
  2867	    private fun createAvatarCharacterBitmap(
  2868	        source: Bitmap,
  2869	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean = false
  2870	    ): Bitmap {
  2871	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2872	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2873	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2874	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2875	
  2876	        val mask = createAvatarCharacterMask(pixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2877	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2878	            if (mask[index]) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  2879	        }
  2880	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2881	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2882	        }
  2883	    }
  2884	
  2885	    private fun createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  2886	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2887	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2888	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2889	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2890	
  2891	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  2892	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  2893	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) * (0.5f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO)
  2894	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  2895	
  2896	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2897	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  2898	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  2899	            val dx = x - centerX
  2900	            val dy = y - centerY
  2901	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy < innerRadiusSquared) {
  2902	                Color.TRANSPARENT
  2903	            } else {
  2904	                pixels[index]
  2905	            }
  2906	        }
  2907	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2908	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2909	        }
  2910	    }
  2911	
  2912	    private fun createAvatarCharacterMask(
  2913	        pixels: IntArray,
  2914	        bitmapWidth: Int,
  2915	        bitmapHeight: Int,
  2916	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2917	    ): BooleanArray {
  2918	        val coloredPixels = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2919	        pixels.forEachIndexed { index, pixel ->
  2920	            coloredPixels[index] = isColorfulAvatarPixel(pixel, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2921	        }
  2922	
  2923	        val mask = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2924	        val outlineRadius = 7
  2925	        for (y in 0 until bitmapHeight) {
  2926	            for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  2927	                val index = y * bitmapWidth + x
  2928	                if (!coloredPixels[index]) continue
  2929	
  2930	                mask[index] = true
  2931	                for (dy in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2932	                    val sampleY = y + dy
  2933	                    if (sampleY !in 0 until bitmapHeight) continue
  2934	                    for (dx in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2935	                        val sampleX = x + dx
  2936	                        if (sampleX !in 0 until bitmapWidth) continue
  2937	                        val sampleIndex = sampleY * bitmapWidth + sampleX
  2938	                        val samplePixel = pixels[sampleIndex]
  2939	                        val sampleAlpha = Color.alpha(samplePixel)
  2940	                        val sampleMax = maxOf(
  2941	                            Color.red(samplePixel),
  2942	                            Color.green(samplePixel),
  2943	                            Color.blue(samplePixel)
  2944	                        )
  2945	                        if (sampleAlpha > 16 && sampleMax <= 44) {
  2946	                            mask[sampleIndex] = true
  2947	                        }
  2948	                    }
  2949	                }
  2950	            }
  2951	        }
  2952	        return mask
  2953	    }
  2954	
  2955	    private fun isColorfulAvatarPixel(
  2956	        pixel: Int,
  2957	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2958	    ): Boolean {
  2959	        val alpha = Color.alpha(pixel)
  2960	        val red = Color.red(pixel)
  2961	        val green = Color.green(pixel)
  2962	        val blue = Color.blue(pixel)
  2963	        val channelMax = maxOf(red, green, blue)
  2964	        val channelMin = minOf(red, green, blue)
  2965	        if (alpha <= 16) return false
  2966	
  2967	        val chroma = channelMax - channelMin
  2968	        return (channelMax > 48 && chroma > 22) ||
  2969	            (includeDarkSaturatedPixels && channelMax > 36 && chroma > 14)
  2970	    }
  2971	
  2972	    private fun standardSignInBackgroundForSize(): Bitmap? {
  2973	        return when {
  2974	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) &&
  2975	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) -> standardLandscapeBackground
  2976	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) &&
  2977	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) -> standardPortraitBackground
  2978	            else -> null
  2979	        }
  2980	    }
  2981	
  2982	    private fun isSizeCloseTo(actual: Int, expected: Int): Boolean {
  2983	        return abs(actual - expected) <= STANDARD_TABLET_SIZE_TOLERANCE
  2984	    }

  2988	            bootProgressAt(IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis) >= 1f
  2989	    }

  2991	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
  2992	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return null
  2993	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return null
  2994	
  2995	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2996	
  2997	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2998	            val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  2999	            val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  3000	            val halfWidth = (avatarSize / 2f + PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_HIT_HORIZONTAL_PADDING) * stage.scale
  3001	            val top = stage.top + portraitAvatarTop() * stage.scale
  3002	            val bottom = stage.top + (
  3003	                portraitProfileNameTop() + portraitProfileNameHeight() +
  3004	                    PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_HIT_BOTTOM_PADDING
  3005	                ) * stage.scale
  3006	            return if (x in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) && y in top..bottom) {
  3007	                FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[portraitProfileIndex].profile
  3008	            } else {
  3009	                null
  3010	            }
  3011	        }
  3012	
  3013	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  3014	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
  3015	            val bounds = target.bounds
  3016	            val centerX = stage.left + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stage.scale
  3017	            val centerY = stage.top + (bounds.y + stackShiftY + bounds.h / 2f) * stage.scale
  3018	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stage.scale / 2f
  3019	            val dx = x - centerX
  3020	            val dy = y - centerY
  3021	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
  3022	        }?.profile
  3023	    }

  3108	    private fun mockSignInFieldHit(x: Float, y: Float): MockSignInField? {
  3109	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null) return null
  3110

  3112	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3113	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3114	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3115	        if (activeMockFlow in listOf(
  3116	                MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount,
  3117	                MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  3118	            ) &&
  3119	            !portraitAuthPointerInViewport(y, baseStage)
  3120	        ) return null
  3121	        val stage = when (activeMockFlow) {
  3122	            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3123	            MockAccountFlow.SignIn -> mockSignInContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3124	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> mockForgotPasswordContentStageMetrics(
  3125	                baseStage,
  3126	                yOffset,
  3127	                focusShiftY
  3128	            )
  3129	            else -> baseStage
  3130	        }
  3131	        return activeMockFields().firstOrNull { field ->
  3132	            val fieldX = mockActiveFieldX(field)
  3133	            val fieldY = mockActiveFieldY(field, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3134	            mockSignInFieldContains(x, y, fieldX, fieldY, stage, mockActiveFieldWidth(field))
  3135	        }
  3136	    }

  3194	    private fun mockDropdownHit(x: Float, y: Float): MockDropdown? {

  3252	    private fun updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3253	        if (!activeMockCreateAvatarDrag) return false
  3254	
  3255	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3256	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockTouchDownY
  3257	        if (
  3258	            !mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  3259	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > touchSlop &&
  3260	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > abs(dragDeltaY)
  3261	        ) {
  3262	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = true
  3263	            lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = if (mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX < 0f) {
  3264	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
  3265	            } else {
  3266	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous
  3267	            }
  3268	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  3269	        }
  3270	
  3271	        if (mockCreateAvatarDragMoved) {
  3272	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3273	        }
  3274	        return true
  3275	    }

  3277	    private fun mockDropdownOptionHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdownOption? {
  3278	        if (
  3279	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  3280	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  3281	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  3282	        ) {
  3283	            return null

  3288	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())

  3290	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3291	        if (!portraitAuthPointerInViewport(pointerY, baseStage)) return null
  3292	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)

  3295	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale

  3300	                (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stage.scale
  3301	            if (pointerX >= scrollbarLeft) return null
  3302	        }
  3303	
  3304	        mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown).forEachIndexed { optionIndex, option ->
  3305	            val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown)
  3306	            val top = stage.top + (yStart + optionIndex * optionHeight) * stage.scale
  3307	            val bottom = top + optionHeight * stage.scale
  3308	            if (pointerY in top..bottom) return MockDropdownOption(dropdown, option)
  3309	        }
  3310	        return null
  3311	    }

  3338	    private fun updateMockDropdownScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3339	        val dropdown = activeMockDropdownScroll ?: return false
  3340	        if (expandedMockDropdown != dropdown || !mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) return false
  3341	
  3342	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  3343	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3344	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3345	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3346	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3347	        val optionHeightPx = (mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  3348	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockDropdownScrollStartY
  3349	        if (abs(dragDeltaY) > touchSlop) {
  3350	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = true
  3351	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  3352	        }
  3353	
  3354	        if (mockDropdownScrollMoved) {
  3355	            val rowDelta = ((mockDropdownScrollStartY - event.y) / optionHeightPx).roundToInt()
  3356	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, mockDropdownScrollStartOffset + rowDelta)
  3357	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3358	        }
  3359	        return true
  3360	    }

  3362	    private fun updatePortraitAuthScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3363	        if (
  3364	            !isPortraitIntroLayout() ||
  3365	            activeMockFlow !in listOf(
  3366	                MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount,
  3367	                MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  3368	            ) ||
  3369	            activeMockDropdownScroll != null
  3370	        ) {
  3371	            return false
  3372	        }
  3373	
  3374	        val deltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3375	        val deltaY = event.y - portraitAuthScrollStartY
  3376	        if (!portraitAuthScrollMoved) {
  3377	            if (abs(deltaY) <= touchSlop || abs(deltaY) <= abs(deltaX)) return false
  3378	            portraitAuthScrollMoved = true
  3379	            pressedMockField = null
  3380	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  3381	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  3382	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  3383	            activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  3384	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  3385	            expandedMockDropdown = null
  3386	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  3387	        }
  3388	
  3389	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  3390	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  3391	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  3392	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  3393	        val maxScrollY = when (activeMockFlow) {
  3394	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> portraitForgotPasswordMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3395	            else -> portraitCreateMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3396	        }
  3397	        portraitAuthScrollY = (portraitAuthScrollStartOffset - deltaY).coerceIn(0f, maxScrollY)
  3398	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3399	        return true
  3400	    }

  3416	    private fun shouldStartMockDragReturn(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3417	        if (mockDragReturnInProgress || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  3418	
  3419	        val upwardDragY = mockTouchDownY - event.y
  3420	        if (upwardDragY <= mockBackDragThresholdPx()) return false
  3421	
  3422	        val dragX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3423	        return upwardDragY > abs(dragX)
  3424	    }

  3428	            MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD * stage.scale

  3430	        return maxOf(stageThreshold, touchSlop * 3f)

  3530	    private fun mockCreateAvatarBounds(columnX: Float, firstFieldY: Float): Bounds {

  3542	    private fun mockCreateAvatarCarouselBounds(
  3543	        columnX: Float,
  3544	        firstFieldY: Float,
  3545	        avatarBounds: Bounds
  3546	    ): Bounds {
  3547	        val centerX = columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f
  3548	        val contentTop = firstFieldY
  3549	        val contentBottom = avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h
  3550	        val contentCenterY = (contentTop + contentBottom) / 2f
  3551	        val chevronSpan = avatarBounds.w +
  3552	            (MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH / 2f) * 2f
  3553	        val side = maxOf(chevronSpan, contentBottom - contentTop)
  3554	        return Bounds(
  3555	            centerX - side / 2f,
  3556	            contentCenterY - side / 2f,
  3557	            side,
  3558	            side
  3559	        )
  3560	    }

  3581	    private fun mockCreateAvatarChevronContains(

  3585	        avatarBounds: Bounds,

  3589	        val centerY = stage.top + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stage.scale

  3591	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale / 2f
  3592	        return pointerX in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) &&
  3593	            pointerY in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)
  3594	    }

  3596	    private fun focusMockSignInField(field: MockSignInField) {
  3597	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = field
  3598	        mockInputDimActiveField = field
  3599	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  3600	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  3601	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  3602	        if (focusedMockField != field) {
  3603	            activeComposingText = ""
  3604	            focusedMockField = field

  3609	        post {
  3610	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  3611	            inputMethodManager?.restartInput(this)
  3612	            inputMethodManager?.showSoftInput(this, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
  3613	        }
  3614	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3615	    }

  3668	    private fun mockSignInContentStageMetrics(
  3669	        stage: StageMetrics,
  3670	        yOffset: Float,
  3671	        focusShiftY: Float
  3672	    ): StageMetrics {

  3674	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  3675	        ) return stage
  3676	
  3677	        val contentScale = stage.scale * portraitCreateContentScale()
  3678	        val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  3679	        val firstFieldY = mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset + focusShiftY
  3680	        val firstFieldTop = stage.top + firstFieldY * stage.scale
  3681	        return StageMetrics(
  3682	            left = centerX - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * contentScale / 2f,
  3683	            top = firstFieldTop - firstFieldY * contentScale,
  3684	            scale = contentScale
  3685	        )
  3686	    }

  3688	    private fun mockForgotPasswordContentStageMetrics(
  3689	        stage: StageMetrics,
  3690	        yOffset: Float,
  3691	        focusShiftY: Float
  3692	    ): StageMetrics {
  3693	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout() || activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) return stage
  3694	
  3695	        val contentScale = stage.scale * portraitCreateContentScale()
  3696	        val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  3697	        val formTopY = mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset + focusShiftY
  3698	        val formTop = stage.top + formTopY * stage.scale - portraitAuthScrollY
  3699	        return StageMetrics(
  3700	            left = centerX - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * contentScale / 2f,
  3701	            top = formTop - formTopY * contentScale,
  3702	            scale = contentScale
  3703	        )
  3704	    }
  3705	
  3706	    private fun portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage: StageMetrics): Float {
  3707	        return stage.top +
  3708	            (portraitLogoVisibleBottom() + MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_VIEWPORT_GAP) * stage.scale
  3709	    }
  3710	
  3711	    private fun portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage: StageMetrics): Float {
  3712	        return height - systemBarInsetBottomPx -
  3713	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_VIEWPORT_BOTTOM_RESERVE * stage.scale
  3714	    }
  3715	
  3716	    private fun portraitAuthPointerInViewport(y: Float, stage: StageMetrics): Boolean {
  3717	        return !isPortraitIntroLayout() ||
  3718	            y in portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage)..portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)
  3719	    }
  3720	
  3721	    private fun mockCreateContentStageMetrics(
  3722	        stage: StageMetrics,
  3723	        yOffset: Float,
  3724	        focusShiftY: Float
  3725	    ): StageMetrics {
  3726	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return stage
  3727	
  3728	        val contentScale = stage.scale * portraitCreateContentScale()
  3729	        val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  3730	        val firstFieldY = mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset + focusShiftY
  3731	        val firstFieldTop = stage.top + firstFieldY * stage.scale - portraitAuthScrollY
  3732	        return StageMetrics(
  3733	            left = centerX - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * contentScale / 2f,
  3734	            top = firstFieldTop - firstFieldY * contentScale,
  3735	            scale = contentScale
  3736	        )
  3737	    }
  3738	
  3739	    private fun portraitCreateMaxScrollY(
  3740	        stage: StageMetrics,
  3741	        yOffset: Float,
  3742	        focusShiftY: Float
  3743	    ): Float {
  3744	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return 0f
  3745	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3746	        val avatarSectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3747	        val avatarBottomY = mockCreateAvatarBounds(
  3748	            mockCreateAvatarColumnX(),
  3749	            avatarSectionY
  3750	        ).let { it.y + it.h }
  3751	        val contentBottom = contentStage.top + avatarBottomY * contentStage.scale +
  3752	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_CONTENT_BOTTOM_GAP * stage.scale + portraitAuthScrollY
  3753	        return (contentBottom - portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  3754	    }
  3755	
  3756	    private fun portraitForgotPasswordMaxScrollY(
  3757	        stage: StageMetrics,
  3758	        yOffset: Float,
  3759	        focusShiftY: Float
  3760	    ): Float {
  3761	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return 0f
  3762	        val contentStage = mockForgotPasswordContentStageMetrics(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3763	        val helpBottomY = mockForgotPasswordHelpBottomY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3764	        val contentBottom = contentStage.top + helpBottomY * contentStage.scale +
  3765	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_CONTENT_BOTTOM_GAP * stage.scale + portraitAuthScrollY
  3766	        return (contentBottom - portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  3767	    }

  3829	    private fun mockForgotRecoverySelectorY(yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {
  3830	        val formTopY = mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset + focusShiftY
  3831	        return if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  3832	            mockForgotPasswordBodyBottomY(formTopY) + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_PORTRAIT_SECTION_GAP
  3833	        } else {
  3834	            formTopY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_TOP_GAP
  3835	        }
  3836	    }

  3838	    private fun mockForgotPasswordButtonY(yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {
  3839	        return mockForgotPasswordFieldY(yOffset, focusShiftY) +
  3840	            MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT +
  3841	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_TOP_GAP

  3843	
  3844	    private fun mockForgotPasswordBodyBottomY(formTopY: Float): Float {
  3845	        val paragraphs = mockForgotPasswordBodyText().split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  3846	        val copyWidth = mockForgotPasswordCopyWidth()
  3847	        var bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  3848	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  3849	            formTopY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_TOP_GAP,
  3850	            copyWidth,
  3851	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  3852	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT
  3853	        )
  3854	        paragraphs.getOrNull(1)?.let { middleText ->
  3855	            bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  3856	                middleText,
  3857	                bottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  3858	                copyWidth,
  3859	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  3860	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT
  3861	            )
  3862	        }
  3863	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  3864	        if (bottomText.isNotEmpty()) {
  3865	            bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  3866	                bottomText,
  3867	                bottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  3868	                copyWidth,
  3869	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  3870	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT
  3871	            )
  3872	        }
  3873	        return bottomY
  3874	    }
  3875	
  3876	    private fun mockForgotPasswordHelpBottomY(yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {
  3877	        val helpTopY = mockForgotPasswordButtonY(yOffset, focusShiftY) +
  3878	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_TOP_GAP
  3879	        return mockParagraphBottomY(
  3880	            mockForgotPasswordHelpText(),
  3881	            helpTopY,
  3882	            mockForgotPasswordFormWidth(),
  3883	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_TEXT_SIZE,
  3884	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_LINE_HEIGHT
  3885	        )
  3886	    }
  3887	
  3888	    private fun mockParagraphBottomY(
  3889	        text: String,
  3890	        topY: Float,
  3891	        maxWidth: Float,
  3892	        textSize: Float,
  3893	        lineHeight: Float
  3894	    ): Float {
  3895	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize
  3896	        return topY + wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth, formInputPaint).size * lineHeight
  3897	    }

  3899	    private fun mockForgotRecoverySelectorLayout(
  3900	        left: Float,
  3901	        width: Float,
  3902	        labelWidth: Float,
  3903	        boxWidth: Float
  3904	    ): ForgotRecoverySelectorLayout {
  3905	        val gap = ((width - labelWidth - boxWidth * 2f) / 4f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  3906	        val labelX = left + gap
  3907	        val usernameBoxX = labelX + labelWidth + gap
  3908	        val passwordBoxX = usernameBoxX + boxWidth + gap
  3909	        return ForgotRecoverySelectorLayout(
  3910	            labelX = labelX,
  3911	            usernameBoxX = usernameBoxX,
  3912	            passwordBoxX = passwordBoxX,
  3913	            boxWidth = boxWidth
  3914	        )
  3915	    }
  3916	
  3917	    private fun mockForgotRecoverySelectorBoxWidth(
  3918	        usernameTextWidth: Float,
  3919	        passwordTextWidth: Float,
  3920	        baseBoxWidth: Float = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_WIDTH
  3921	    ): Float {
  3922	        val widestTextWidth = maxOf(usernameTextWidth, passwordTextWidth)
  3923	        val currentHorizontalPadding = ((baseBoxWidth - widestTextWidth) / 2f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  3924	        return widestTextWidth + currentHorizontalPadding * 4f
  3925	    }

  3935	    private fun mockActiveFieldX(field: MockSignInField): Float {
  3936	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) return mockForgotPasswordFormX()
  3937	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return mockFormX()
  3938	
  3939	        val firstColumnX = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) mockCreatePortraitColumnX() else mockCreateFormX()
  3940	        val middleColumnX = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  3941	            mockCreatePortraitColumnX()
  3942	        } else {
  3943	            mockCreateFormX() + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH + MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMN_GAP
  3944	        }
  3945	        return when (field) {
  3946	            MockSignInField.Username,
  3947	            MockSignInField.Password,
  3948	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> firstColumnX
  3949	            MockSignInField.Email -> middleColumnX
  3950	            MockSignInField.Month -> middleColumnX
  3951	            MockSignInField.Day -> middleColumnX + MOCK_DATE_MONTH_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_FIELD_GAP
  3952	            MockSignInField.Year -> middleColumnX + MOCK_DATE_MONTH_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DAY_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_FIELD_GAP * 2f
  3953	        }
  3954	    }
  3955	
  3956	    private fun mockActiveFieldY(field: MockSignInField, yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {
  3957	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  3958	            return mockForgotPasswordFieldY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3959	        }
  3960	
  3961	        val rowIndex = if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  3962	            when (field) {
  3963	                MockSignInField.Username,
  3964	                MockSignInField.Email -> 0
  3965	                MockSignInField.Password -> 1
  3966	                MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword,
  3967	                MockSignInField.Month,
  3968	                MockSignInField.Day,
  3969	                MockSignInField.Year -> 2
  3970	            }
  3971	        } else {
  3972	            activeMockFields().indexOf(field).coerceAtLeast(0)
  3973	        }
  3974	        val usesSecondSection = activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  3975	            isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  3976	            field in listOf(
  3977	                MockSignInField.Email,
  3978	                MockSignInField.Month,
  3979	                MockSignInField.Day,
  3980	                MockSignInField.Year
  3981	            )
  3982	        val sectionTop = if (usesSecondSection) {
  3983	            mockCreateSecondSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  3984	        } else {
  3985	            mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset + focusShiftY
  3986	        }
  3987	        return sectionTop + rowIndex * (MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_GAP)
  3988	    }

  4104	    private fun mockDropdownUsesDateViewport(dropdown: MockDropdown): Boolean {
  4105	        return dropdown != MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier
  4106	    }
  4107	
  4108	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  4109	        val optionCount = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size
  4110	        val viewportCount = if (mockDropdownUsesDateViewport(dropdown)) {
  4111	            MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_VISIBLE_OPTION_COUNT
  4112	        } else {
  4113	            optionCount
  4114	        }
  4115	        return min(optionCount, viewportCount)
  4116	    }
  4117	
  4118	    private fun mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown): Boolean {
  4119	        return mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size > mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  4120	    }
  4121	
  4122	    private fun mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  4123	        return (mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] ?: 0).coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  4124	    }
  4125	
  4126	    private fun mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  4127	        return (mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size - mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)).coerceAtLeast(0)
  4128	    }
  4129	
  4130	    private fun setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown, offset: Int) {
  4131	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = offset.coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  4132	    }
  4133	
  4134	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown: MockDropdown): List<String> {
  4135	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  4136	        val offset = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown)
  4137	        return options.drop(offset).take(mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown))
  4138	    }
  4139	
  4140	    private fun prepareMockDropdownScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown) {
  4141	        if (!mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  4142	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, 0)
  4143	            return
  4144	        }
  4145	
  4146	        val selectedIndex = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).indexOf(mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown))
  4147	        setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, if (selectedIndex >= 0) selectedIndex else 0)
  4148	    }

  4150	    private fun mockDropdownDisplayText(dropdown: MockDropdown): String {
  4151	        return when (dropdown) {
  4152	            MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier -> normalizedMembershipTierSelection(subscriptionTierText)
  4153	            else -> mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown)
  4154	        }
  4155	    }

  4159	            MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier -> normalizedMembershipTierSelection(subscriptionTierText)
  4160	            MockDropdown.Month -> monthText
  4161	            MockDropdown.Day -> dayText
  4162	            MockDropdown.Year -> yearText
  4163	        }
  4164	    }

  4166	    private fun mockDropdownOptions(dropdown: MockDropdown): List<String> {

  4169	            MockDropdown.Month -> MOCK_MONTH_OPTIONS

  4421	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  4422	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  4423	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  4424	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4425	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  4426	        return mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize), stage.scale)
  4427	    }

  4432	    }

  4511	            mockInputDimActiveField = if (expandedMockDropdown == null) focusedMockField else null
  4512	        }
  4513

  4515	        val elapsed = (now - lastMockInputDimAnimationMillis).coerceIn(0L, 48L)
  4516	        lastMockInputDimAnimationMillis = now
  4517	        val step = elapsed / MOCK_INPUT_DIM_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat()
  4518	        mockInputDimProgress = moveToward(mockInputDimProgress, target, step)
  4519	
  4520	        if (mockInputDimProgress == 0f && target == 0f) {
  4521	            mockInputDimActiveField = null
  4522	            mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  4523	        }
  4524	    }

  4561	    private fun mockLandscapeInputLiftTarget(): Float {
  4562	        if (width <= height) return 0f
  4563	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  4564	        return if (
  4565	            focusedMockField != null ||
  4566	            expandedMockDropdown != null ||
  4567	            isSoftKeyboardVisible()
  4568	        ) {
  4569	            1f
  4570	        } else {
  4571	            0f
  4572	        }
  4573	    }
  4574	
  4575	    private fun mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale: Float): Float {
  4576	        if (width <= height || mockSignInStartMillis == null) return 0f
  4577	        val liftMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockLandscapeInputLiftProgress)
  4578	        return MOCK_FORM_LANDSCAPE_FOCUS_SHIFT_Y * stageScale * liftMotion
  4579	    }
  4580	
  4581	    private fun isSoftKeyboardVisible(): Boolean {
  4582	        val rootInsets = ViewCompat.getRootWindowInsets(this)
  4583	        if (rootInsets != null) {
  4584	            val imeInsets = rootInsets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime())
  4585	            if (rootInsets.isVisible(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime()) || imeInsets.bottom > 0) return true
  4586	        }
  4587	
  4588	        val rootHeight = rootView?.height ?: 0
  4589	        if (rootHeight <= 0) return false
  4590	        getWindowVisibleDisplayFrame(tempWindowRect)
  4591	        val obscuredHeight = rootHeight - tempWindowRect.height()
  4592	        return obscuredHeight > rootHeight * 0.18f
  4593	    }

  4599	        return if (focusedMockField == awaitingField) 1f else 0f
  4600	    }

  4609	            focusedMockField != null ||
  4610	            activeMockFields().any { mockSignInFieldText(it).isNotEmpty() }

  4675	            MockSignInField.Username -> usernameText
  4676	            MockSignInField.Password -> "*".repeat(passwordText.length)
  4677	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> "*".repeat(confirmPasswordText.length)
  4678	            MockSignInField.Email -> emailText
  4679	            MockSignInField.Month -> monthText
  4680	            MockSignInField.Day -> dayText
  4681	            MockSignInField.Year -> yearText
  4682	        }
  4683	    }
  4684	
  4685	    private fun setMockSignInFieldText(field: MockSignInField, text: String) {
  4686	        val limitedText = text.take(MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS)
  4687	        when (field) {
  4688	            MockSignInField.Username -> usernameText = limitedText
  4689	            MockSignInField.Password -> passwordText = limitedText
  4690	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> confirmPasswordText = limitedText
  4691	            MockSignInField.Email -> emailText = limitedText
  4692	            MockSignInField.Month -> monthText = limitedText
  4693	            MockSignInField.Day -> dayText = limitedText
  4694	            MockSignInField.Year -> yearText = limitedText
  4695	        }
  4696	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword && field == MockSignInField.Email) {
  4697	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  4698	        }
  4699	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  4700	        if (mockInputDimAwaitingField == field) {
  4701	            mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  4702	        }
  4703	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  4704	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  4705	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  4706	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4707	    }
  4708	
  4709	    private fun appendMockSignInInput(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  4710	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  4711	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  4712	        if (input.isEmpty()) return true
  4713	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  4714	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  4715	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  4716	        val insertedText = input.take(availableChars)
  4717	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + insertedText)
  4718	        activeComposingText = ""
  4719	        return true
  4720	    }
  4721	
  4722	    private fun replaceMockSignInComposingText(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  4723	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  4724	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  4725	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  4726	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  4727	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  4728	        val composingText = input.take(availableChars)
  4729	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + composingText)
  4730	        activeComposingText = composingText
  4731	        return true
  4732	    }
  4733	
  4734	    private fun finishMockSignInComposingText(): Boolean {
  4735	        activeComposingText = ""
  4736	        return true
  4737	    }
  4738	
  4739	    private fun deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength: Int): Boolean {
  4740	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  4741	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  4742	        if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
  4743	            activeComposingText = ""
  4744	            return true
  4745	        }
  4746	        val deleteCount = beforeLength.coerceAtLeast(1).coerceAtMost(currentText.length)
  4747	        activeComposingText = ""
  4748	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, currentText.dropLast(deleteCount))
  4749	        return true
  4750	    }
  4751	
  4752	    private fun handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  4753	        activeComposingText = ""
  4754	        val fields = activeMockFields()
  4755	        val fieldIndex = fields.indexOf(focusedMockField)
  4756	        if (fieldIndex >= 0 && fieldIndex < fields.lastIndex && actionCode != EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE) {
  4757	            focusMockSignInField(fields[fieldIndex + 1])
  4758	        } else if (
  4759	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  4760	            focusedMockField == MockSignInField.Email &&
  4761	            mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  4762	        ) {
  4763	            startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  4764	        } else {
  4765	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  4766	            inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  4767	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  4768	        }
  4769	        return true
  4770	    }
  4771	
  4772	    private fun handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  4773	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  4774	        return when (keyCode) {
  4775	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  4776	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  4777	            else -> {
  4778	                val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  4779	                typedCharacter?.let(::appendMockSignInInput) ?: false
  4780	            }
  4781	        }
  4782	    }
  4783	
  4784	    private fun sanitizeMockSignInInput(input: String): String {
  4785	        return buildString {
  4786	            input.forEach { character ->
  4787	                if (character != '\n' && character != '\r' && !character.isISOControl()) {
  4788	                    append(character)
  4789	                }
  4790	            }
  4791	        }
  4792	    }
  4793	
  4794	    private fun trailingFittingText(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): String {
  4795	        if (paint.measureText(text) <= maxWidth) return text
  4796	
  4797	        var startIndex = 0
  4798	        while (startIndex < text.length && paint.measureText(text.substring(startIndex)) > maxWidth) {
  4799	            startIndex += 1
  4800	        }
  4801	        return text.substring(startIndex)
  4802	    }
  4803	
  4804	    private fun wrappedTextLines(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): List<String> {
  4805	        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
  4806	        text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n")).forEachIndexed { paragraphIndex, paragraph ->
  4807	            if (paragraphIndex > 0) {
  4808	                lines += ""
  4809	            }
  4810	
  4811	            var currentLine = ""
  4812	            paragraph.replace('\n', ' ')
  4813	                .split(Regex("\\s+"))
  4814	                .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
  4815	                .forEach { word ->
  4816	                    val candidateLine = if (currentLine.isEmpty()) word else "$currentLine $word"
  4817	                    if (currentLine.isEmpty() || paint.measureText(candidateLine) <= maxWidth) {
  4818	                        currentLine = candidateLine
  4819	                    } else {
  4820	                        lines += currentLine
  4821	                        currentLine = word
  4822	                    }
  4823	                }
  4824	            if (currentLine.isNotEmpty()) {
  4825	                lines += currentLine
  4826	            }
  4827	        }
  4828	        return lines
  4829	    }
  4830	
  4831	    private fun moveToward(current: Float, target: Float, amount: Float): Float {
  4832	        return when {
  4833	            current < target -> (current + amount).coerceAtMost(target)
  4834	            current > target -> (current - amount).coerceAtLeast(target)
  4835	            else -> current
  4836	        }
  4837	    }
  4838	
  4839	    private fun currentStageMetrics(): StageMetrics? {
  4840	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return null
  4841	
  4842	        val standardScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  4843	        val portraitScale = portraitDesignScale()
  4844	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  4845	        val stageScale = lerpFloat(standardScale, portraitScale, portraitAmount)
  4846	        val standardTop = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * standardScale) / 2f
  4847	        return StageMetrics(
  4848	            left = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f,
  4849	            top = lerpFloat(standardTop, 0f, portraitAmount) +
  4850	                mockAuthOpenStageLiftY(stageScale) +
  4851	                mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale),
  4852	            scale = stageScale
  4853	        )
  4854	    }
  4855	
  4856	    private fun isPortraitIntroLayout(): Boolean = width <= height
  4857	
  4858	    private fun portraitIntroAmount(): Float {
  4859	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return 0f
  4860	        // Keep the portrait welcome composition fixed while an auth panel slides in.
  4861	        return 1f
  4862	    }
  4863	
  4864	    private fun portraitDesignScale(): Float {
  4865	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return 1f
  4866	
  4867	        val widthScale = width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
  4868	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return widthScale
  4869	
  4870	        val referenceDesignHeight = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * PORTRAIT_REFERENCE_HEIGHT_TO_WIDTH
  4871	        return min(widthScale, height / referenceDesignHeight)
  4872	    }

  4878	    }

  4881	        val top = portraitSafeTop()
  4882	        return top + (portraitSafeBottom() - top) * fraction
  4883	    }

  4987	            (rawForgotCenter + PORTRAIT_PROMPT_VISIBLE_HALF_HEIGHT)
  4988	        return (rawTopRoom + rawBottomRoom) / 2f

  4994	            (PORTRAIT_COMFORTABLE_DESIGN_HEIGHT - PORTRAIT_COMPACT_DESIGN_HEIGHT))
  4995	            .coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4996	    }

  5068	    private fun switchMockAccountFlow(flow: MockAccountFlow) {
  5069	        if (
  5070	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  5071	            activeMockFlow == flow ||
  5072	            isMockSignInClosing() ||
  5073	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()
  5074	        ) {
  5075	            return
  5076	        }
  5077	
  5078	        val previousFlow = activeMockFlow ?: return
  5079	        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  5080	        activeComposingText = ""
  5081	        expandedMockDropdown = null
  5082	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  5083	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  5084	        portraitAuthScrollY = 0f
  5085	        portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
  5086	        pressedMockField = null
  5087	        pressedRememberMe = false
  5088	        pressedBackChevron = false
  5089	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  5090	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  5091	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  5092	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  5093	        lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  5094	        pressedMockDropdown = null
  5095	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  5096	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  5097	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  5098	        resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  5099	        if (flow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  5100	            forgotRecoveryTarget = MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
  5101	            emailText = ""
  5102	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  5103	        }
  5104	        outgoingMockFlow = previousFlow
  5105	        activeMockFlow = flow
  5106	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  5107	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  5108	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  5109	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  5110	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  5111	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  5112	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  5113	    }

  5204	    private fun mockSignInProgress(): Float {
  5205	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return 0f
  5206	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  5207	        val progress = (elapsed / mockSignInTransitionDurationMs().toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5208	        return lerpFloat(
  5209	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress,
  5210	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress,
  5211	            progress
  5212	        )
  5213	    }
  5214	
  5215	    private fun mockFlowSwitchProgress(): Float {
  5216	        val startMillis = mockFlowSwitchStartMillis ?: return 1f
  5217	        return ((SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis) / MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat())
  5218	            .coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5219	    }
  5220	
  5221	    private fun isMockFlowSwitchAnimating(): Boolean {
  5222	        return mockFlowSwitchStartMillis != null && mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f
  5223	    }
  5224	
  5225	    private fun finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded() {
  5226	        if (mockFlowSwitchStartMillis == null || mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f) return
  5227	        outgoingMockFlow = null
  5228	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = null
  5229	    }
  5230	
  5231	    private fun isMockSignInAnimating(): Boolean {
  5232	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return false
  5233	        return SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()
  5234	    }
  5235	
  5236	    private fun isMockSignInClosing(): Boolean {
  5237	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f
  5238	    }
  5239	
  5240	    private fun mockSignInTransitionDurationMs(): Long {
  5241	        val distance = abs(mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress - mockSignInTransitionStartProgress)
  5242	        return (MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS * distance)
  5243	            .roundToInt()
  5244	            .coerceAtLeast(1)
  5245	            .toLong()
  5246	    }
  5247	
  5248	    private fun finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded() {
  5249	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return
  5250	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()) return
  5251	
  5252	        if (mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f) {
  5253	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
  5254	            activeMockFlow = null
  5255	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
  5256	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  5257	            pressedMockField = null
  5258	            pressedRememberMe = false
  5259	            pressedBackChevron = false
  5260	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  5261	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  5262	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  5263	            pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  5264	            pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  5265	            activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  5266	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  5267	            mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  5268	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  5269	            resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  5270	            notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  5271	            return
  5272	        }
  5273	
  5274	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
  5275	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  5276	    }

  5278	    private inner class MockSignInInputConnection : BaseInputConnection(this@CinerificIntroView, false) {
  5279	        override fun commitText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  5280	            return appendMockSignInInput(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  5281	        }

  5297	        }

  5299	        override fun getTextBeforeCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  5300	            val text = focusedMockField?.let(::mockSignInFieldText).orEmpty()

  5302	        }

  5305	            return ""
  5306	        }

  5309	            return ""
  5310	        }

  5312	        override fun performEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  5313	            return handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode)
  5314	        }
  5315	
  5316	        override fun sendKeyEvent(event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  5317	            if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  5318	            return when (event.keyCode) {
  5319	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  5320	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  5321	                else -> {
  5322	                    val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  5323	                    if (typedCharacter != null) {
  5324	                        appendMockSignInInput(typedCharacter)
  5325	                    } else {
  5326	                        super.sendKeyEvent(event)
  5327	                    }
  5328	                }
  5329	            }
  5330	        }
  5331	    }
  5332	}
